> Ntụaka zuru ezu maka environment variable ọ bụla OmniRoute na-amata.
> Maka template mbido-ngwa-ngwa, lee [`.env.example`](../../.env.example).

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Variable ọ bụla edepụtara ebe a ga-apụtakwa na `.env.example`, variable ọ bụla dị na
> `.env.example` ga-apụtakwa ebe a. `npm run check:env-doc-sync`
> na-amanye nke a mgbe a na-eme commit nakwa na CI. Iji kpachapụ variable ụma, tinye ya na
> allowlist dị n'ime `scripts/check/check-env-doc-sync.mjs`.

## 1. Ihe Nzuzo Ndị A Chọrọ

A **ga-**ahazi ihe ndị a tupu mmalite mbụ. Na-enweghị ha, ngwa ahụ ga-ajụ ịmalite ma ọ bụ jiri ntọala ndabara na-enweghị nchebe rụọ ọrụ.

| Ihe Mgbanwe                  | Achọrọ Ya        | Ndabara                          | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                         | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ---------------------------- | ---------------- | -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `JWT_SECRET`                 | **Ee**           | _(ọ dịghị)_                      | `src/lib/auth`                                            | Na-eji mbinye aka echekwa/nyochaa kuki nnọkọ dashboard niile (JWT). Jiri `openssl rand -base64 48` mepụta ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `API_KEY_SECRET`             | **Ee**           | _(ọ dịghị)_                      | `src/lib/db/apiKeys.ts`                                   | Igodo nzuzo AES maka ụkpụrụ igodo API echekwara na SQLite. Jiri `openssl rand -hex 32` mepụta ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `INITIAL_PASSWORD`           | **Ee**           | `CHANGEME`                       | Skripụ mbido                                              | Na-edobe okwuntughe mbụ nke dashboard onye nchịkwa (ọ dabara na ndabara `.env.example` — e mere ka o doo anya na ọ naghị echebe nke ọma iji manye ka a gbanwee ya). **Gbanwee ya tupu ojiji mbụ.** Mgbe ịbanyechara, gbanwee ya site na Dashboard → Settings → Security.                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET` | **Ee** (mmepụta) | _(edoghị ya)_                    | `src/app/api/internal/codex-responses-ws/route.ts`        | Nzuzo a na-ekekọrịta maka àkwà mmiri WebSocket nke Codex Responses dị n'ime. Ọ na-enyocha arịrịọ àkwà mmiri dị n'etiti nnyefe WS nke Electron/ihe nchọgharị na OmniRoute. ⚠️ **A CHỌRỌ YA na mmepụta — mgbe edoghị ya, a na-ajụ arịrịọ àkwà mmiri WS niile.** Jiri `openssl rand -base64 32` mepụta ya.                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_SW_BUILD_ID`      | Mba              | _(git SHA)_                      | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | ID doro anya maka imebi cache nke service-worker maka shell PWA (#11779); ọ bụ nke mbụ n'usoro mkpebi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `SOURCE_VERSION`             | Mba              | _(edoghị ya)_                    | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Nke abụọ n'usoro ahụ — ndị nrụpụta PaaS (dịka ụdị Heroku) na-edobe ya ka ọ bụrụ commit e bugoro.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_SW_BUILD_ID`    | Mba              | _(e sitere na ndị ọzọ nweta ya)_ | `src/shared/components/PwaRegister.tsx`                   | Uru ọha nke oge nrụpụta nke klayent na-eji edebanye `/sw.js?v=…`; a na-enweta ya site na abụọ dị n'elu, emesịa site na git SHA.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_STAMP_TOKEN` | Mba (akpaka)     | _(akpaka n'oge mbido ọ bụla)_    | `src/server/authz/policies/management.ts`                 | Nzuzo nke usoro ọ bụla nke na-egosi na akara peer-IP a tụkwasịrị obi sitere na sava HTTP nke OmniRoute n'onwe ya (`scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`). Middleware authz na-atụkwasị obi na arịrịọ sitere na mpaghara ahụ (mgbochi loopback/LAN nke ụzọ LOCAL_ONLY) naanị mgbe akara ahụ nwere token a. A na-emepụta ya na-akpaghị aka n'oge mbido ọ bụla — hapụ ya ka edoghị ya; debe ya naanị maka nhazi nwere ọtụtụ usoro ndị ga-ekekọrịta akara ahụ. |

### Iwu Mmepụta

```bash
# Mepụta nzuzo anọ niile n'otu oge:
echo "JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)"
echo "API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)"
echo "INITIAL_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -base64 16)"
echo "OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 32)"
```

> [!CAUTION]
> Etinyela faịlụ `.env` nwere ezigbo nzuzo na njikwa ụdị. `.gitignore` ewepụla `.env`, mana nyochaa ya tupu iziga mgbanwe.

---

## 2. Nchekwa & Ebe Nchekwa Data

OmniRoute na-eji **SQLite** (site na `better-sqlite3`) maka nchekwa data niile na-adịgide adịgide. Ndị mgbanwe ndị a na-achịkwa ebe data dị, izo ya ezo, na usoro ndụ ya.

| Mgbanwe                                    | Ndabara                       | Faịlụ Isi                                             | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `DATA_DIR`                                 | `~/.omniroute/`               | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Ndekọ mgbọrọgwụ maka DB SQLite, nkwado ndabere, na faịlụ data. Gbanwee ya maka voluum Docker ma ọ bụ ụzọ ahaziri iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_DEFAULT_DATA_DIR`         | _(edobeghị)_                  | `src/lib/dataPaths.ts`                                | Ụzọ mgbapụ maka ihe nche DATA_DIR nke nnwale/eval (#10428). A na-ebuga nnwale na nyocha eval/print nke Node (`-e`/`--eval`/`-p`/`--print`, gụnyere ụdị `--eval=`/`--print=`) ndị na-enweghị `DATA_DIR` gaa na ndekọ temp a ga-atụfu ka ha ghara imepe ebe nchekwa data n'ezie nke onye na-ahụ maka sistemụ; debe ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji họrọ iji ndekọ n'ezie ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_SHA`                      | _(edobeghị)_                  | `src/lib/monitoring/buildSha.ts`                      | Git SHA nke artifact na-arụ ọrụ. `npm run build:release` na-akanye ya; enwere ike itinye ya n'ime containers ndị na-enweghị akara `dist/BUILD_SHA`. A na-egosi ya dịka `system.buildSha` na `/api/monitoring/health`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELEASE_REF`                    | `origin/main`                 | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Ref nke ọnụ ụzọ nyocha mmalite artifact a kwakọbara ji atụnyere SHA nke build (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CANARY_BUILD`             | _(edobeghị)_                  | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Debe ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji kwe ka a kwakọta build nke SHA ya adịghị n'ahịrị mwepụta, na-edekọ ya dịka canary e kpachapụrụ anya kama ime ka ọnụ ụzọ nyocha daa (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_API_KEY`                  | _(edobeghị)_                  | `scripts/ops/deploy-canary.mjs`                       | Igodo API maka nyocha smoke nke nkenye canary, nke a na-eziga dịka `Authorization: Bearer` na `/v1/chat/completions`. Naanị skript nkenye na-eji ya (#10429), sava anaghị eji ya. Ọ nweghị njikọ na mgbanwe `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_*` nke ngwa nnwale smoke CLI a ga-ahọrọ isonye na ya (`RUN_CLI_SMOKE=1`, `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_BASE_URL/MODEL/API_KEY_ENV/TARGETS/TIMEOUT_MS` na `tests/integration/upstream-cli-smoke.int.test.ts`) — lee [Njikọ CLI → Nnyocha smoke n'ezie](../guides/CLI-INTEGRATIONS.md).                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILDING`                       | _(edobeghị)_                  | `src/lib/buildPhase.ts`                               | Ihe mgbaama oge build (#10060): `scripts/build/build-next-isolated.mjs` na-edobe ya ka ọ bụrụ `1`, onye ọrụ build ọ bụla e mepụtara na-eketakwa ya ka oyi akwa DB weghachite stub na-adịghị eme ihe kama ibunye addon better-sqlite3 nke native (nke na-akwụsị onye ọrụ ahụ mgbe ọ na-apụ). A naghị edobe ya mgbe sava na-arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_NATIVE_DEP_CHECK`          | `0`                           | `scripts/check/check-native-deps.mjs`                 | Debe ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji mafere nyocha prebuild nke dependency native nhọrọ maka osisi vendored pụrụ iche. Nke a anaghị eme ka dependency na-efu nwee ike ịrụ build; jiri ya naanị mgbe a na-enye dependencies native site n'ụzọ ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR`                       | _(edobeghị)_                  | `open-sse/executors/promptql/threadSticky.ts`         | **Aha nnọchi fallback** maka `DATA_DIR`, nke a na-enyocha naanị mgbe edobeghị `DATA_DIR`. A na-eji ya achọta cache session thread-sticky nke PromptQL executor dị na diski (`<dir>/promptql-thread-sessions.json`); ọ bụrụ na edobeghị mgbanwe abụọ ahụ, cache ahụ ga-anọ naanị na ebe nchekwa RAM (agaghị echekwa ya n'ofe mmalitegharị).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR`                    | _(edobeghị)_                  | `src/lib/plugins/scanner.ts`                          | Ndekọ nke **runtime plugin scanner** na-agụ — nakwa mgbọrọgwụ ebe plugin manager na-etinye ihe — na-anọchi ndabara sitere na home (#11827). Tụọ ya aka na osisi plugin e ji bind-mount tinye na Docker/K8s kama ịkwaga HOME naanị iji gbanwee ebe ụzọ nyocha dị (HOME na-achịkwa omume ndị ọzọ niile metụtara home kwa). Mgbe edobeghị ya = `~/.omniroute/plugins`, ma ọ bụ `/tmp/.omniroute/plugins` mgbe process ahụ anaghị ebupụ home ma ọlị — nke a bụ nsogbu nchọpụta na-emeghị nwayọ nke mgbanwe a na-ewepụ. A na-edekọ ndekọ e kpebiri otu ugboro mgbe usoro malitere dịka `scanner.dir_resolved`, tinyere input nke meriri. Maka akụkụ sava naanị: plugins iwu CLI na-edobe `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH` nke ha (ngalaba 9). |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`                   | _(efu = agbanyụrụ)_           | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | Igodo AES maka izobe nchekwa data SQLite niile mgbe echekwara ya. Jiri `openssl rand -hex 32` mepụta ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY_VERSION`           | `v1`                          | `scripts/build/bootstrap-env.mjs`, `electron/main.js` | Akara ụdị maka igodo nzuzo ahụ. Bulie ya mgbe ị na-eme ntụgharị igodo iji kwado ịtọpụ nzuzo ndabere ochie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`               | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/backup.ts`                                | Mgbe ọ bụ `true`, ọ na-amafere ndabere faịlụ SQLite a na-eme mgbe niile/tupu ide ihe (ichekwa/ihichapụ ọnụahịa models.dev, ide ntọala). Ndabere aka na nke tupu mweghachi ka na-arụ ọrụ. Ọ **dịghị** agbanyụ foto nchekwa na-adịgide adịgide nke iwu mbufe chọrọ ma ọ bụ ihe mgbochi mbufe buru ibu maka DB na-adịgide adịgide dị adị. A na-egbochi ndabere ndị na-abụghị nke aka ka ha ghara ịrụ ọrụ karịa otu ugboro n'ime nkeji 60. **Settings → Storage** nke Dashboard nwere ike ịgbanyụ ndabere akpaaka a na-eme mgbe niile n'onwe ya.                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CRYPT_KEY`                      | _(edobeghị)_                  | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Aha nnọchi ochie** maka `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`. A na-anabata ya dị ka nhọrọ ndabere mgbe mgbanwe bụ isi adịghị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_BASE64`                 | _(edobeghị)_                  | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Aha nnọchi ochie** (ụdị e tinyere koodu Base64) a na-anabata dị ka nhọrọ ndabere. A na-ewepụ koodu ya na-akpaghị aka tupu eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_DB_HEALTHCHECK_INTERVAL_MS`     | _(edobeghị)_                  | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Gbanwee nkeji oge nyocha ahụike SQLite a na-eme mgbe niile (ms). Mgbe edobeghị ya, a na-enweta ụkpụrụ ndabara site na `NODE_ENV`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`       | `21600000` (6h)               | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Gbanwee nkeji oge `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` a na-eme mgbe niile (ms). Nlele akpaaka anaghị ebelata faịlụ WAL n'onwe ya, sava na-arụ ọrụ ogologo oge anaghịkwa emechi DB ya. `0` na-agbanyụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_PASSIVE_INTERVAL_MS`        | `300000` (5m)                 | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Gbanwee nkeji oge `wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE)` a na-eme ugboro ugboro (ms). Ọ na-eme ka fremụ WAL ndị na-eche ka dị ntakịrị, ka TRUNCATE a na-eme mgbe niile ghara idetuo ọrụ gbakọtara nke ọtụtụ GB na thread bụ isi. `0` na-agbanyụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_GUARD_MAX_MB`               | `256`                         | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Mgbe okirikiri PASSIVE hụrụ na faịlụ WAL karịrị nha a, ọ na-ebuli ya ozugbo gaa na `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` kama ichere okirikiri nwayọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` iji kwụsị usoro ahụ mgbe nrụgide akụ dị oke egwu nọgidere ruo `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS`, nke na-enye onye nlekọta (systemd `Restart=always`, amụma mmalitegharị Docker) ohere iweghachite usoro dị ọcha kama ịnọgide na-enye 503 ruo mgbe ebighị ebi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS` | `120000` (2m)                 | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Ogologo oge nrụgide dị oke egwu ga-adịgide tupu nkwụsị mmalitegharị onwe ya amalite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLJS_WASM_PATH`                | _(nchọpụta akpaaka)_          | `src/lib/db/adapters/sqljsAdapter.ts`                 | Ụzọ doro anya (zuru ezu ma ọ bụ dabere na cwd) gaa na `sql-wasm.wasm` mgbe a na-eji ihe nkwụnye ndabere WASM `sql.js`. Mgbe edobeghị ya, a na-achọpụta ya na-akpaghị aka site na ndabere ngwugwu na nhazi ndị a tụrụ aro.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`       | `21600000` (6h)               | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Gbanwee nkeji oge `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` a na-eme mgbe niile (ms). Nlele akpaaka anaghị ebelata faịlụ WAL n'onwe ya, sava na-arụ ọrụ ogologo oge anaghịkwa emechi DB ya. `0` na-agbanyụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`           | `30`                          | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Ụbọchị a na-edobe ebe nlele ọrụ Batch API kwụsịrị (emechara/dara/akagbuola/kubiela), faịlụ ntinye/mmepụta/mperi ndị e zoro aka na ha, na ahịrị ya site na mkpocha akpaaka tupu ihichapụ ha. Ọ na-amalite ịrụ ọrụ naanị mgbe agbanyere `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`; ọ dabara na oge njide mmepụta Batch API nke OpenAI n'onwe ya. Ọ naghị emetụta ụzọ `DELETE /api/v1/batches/delete-completed` onye njikwa na-akpalite, nke na-anọgide na-enweghị ọnọdụ ọ bụla (enweghị nzacha afọ) dịka e siri hazie ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`      | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Mgbe ọ bụ `true`, kwe ka nhichapụ akpaka hichapụ ọrụ Batch API ndị rurula njedebe (yana ebe-nchekwa ọnọdụ ha) gafere `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`, ma kpochapụ ọdịnaya BLOB nke faịlụ ndị ebulitere mgbe ha gafere `expires_at` nke ha. Agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara: nrụnye ọ bụla dị ugbu a ga-edobe data a otu o si dịbu ruo mgbe onye nchịkwa họọrọ ịgbanye ya. Ọ bụkwa ọkọlọtọ atụmatụ enwere ike idezi na dashboard — lee `docs/reference/FEATURE_FLAGS.md` → Oge nrụọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`            | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`, `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`     | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji wụfee nyocha ahụike DB kpamkpam mgbe mmalite. Ọ bara uru maka ọrụ ndị na-adị obere oge na ule njikọta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORCE_DB_HEALTHCHECK`           | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji manye loop nyocha ahụike DB ka ọ rụọ ọrụ, ọbụlagodi mgbe a gaara awụfee ya n'ọnọdụ nkịtị (dịka ọmụmaatụ, ọrụ ndị na-adị obere oge).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_POSTINSTALL`               | `0`                           | `scripts/postinstall.mjs`                             | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji wụfee nkwadebe runtime nke sistemụ mgbe `npm install` na-aga. Ọ bara uru na nrụnye CI/ndị na-enweghị ihuenyo ebe arụworị sqlite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_MIGRATIONS_DIR`                 | _(achọpụta na-akpaghị aka)_   | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Dochie ndekọ ebe migration runner na-enyocha. Ọ bara uru mgbe a na-ebupu migrations agbakọtara ọnụ n'ime build ahaziri iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_EXTRA_MIGRATIONS_DIRS`          | _(atọghị)_                    | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner/extraDirs.ts`             | Ndekọ migration ndị ọzọ dị ka ntinye `namespace=dir` nke ihe nkewa path nke platform kewara (dịka ọmụmaatụ `ee=/opt/app/enterprise/db/migrations`). A na-edekọ faịlụ ndị a hụrụ ebe ahụ dị ka `<namespace>-<number>`, ka nkesa na-ebupu migrations nke ya ghara ịdakọrịta na oghere ọnụọgụ nke upstream. Ntinye na-ezighi ezi, namespace na-adịghị irè, ma ọ bụ ndekọ na-adịghị adị ga-ebute njehie mgbe mmalite kama ịwụfee schema na nzuzo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_PENDING_MIGRATIONS`         | `50`                          | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Oke nchekwa maka ọtụtụ migrations na-eche (#3416). Mmalite ga-akwụsị ma ọ bụrụ na migrations na-eche karịrị ọnụ ọgụgụ a na DB dị ugbu a (nke a na-echebe megide tebụl nsuso ehichapụrụ). Welie ya iji weghachite backup ochie; tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ nyocha ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTALL_UPGRADE_WORKDIR`        | _(`<repo>/.install-upgrade`)_ | `scripts/check/check-install-upgrade.mjs`             | Ndekọ ọrụ maka ọnụ ụzọ nnwapụta mwepụta `check:install-upgrade`. Ọ chọrọ ihe dịka 12 GB (osisi nrụnye abụọ nke ọ bụla dị ihe dịka 3 GB tinyere tarball), ya mere, ọ gaghị arụ ọrụ na tmpfs pere mpe — na runner nke a na-akwado n'onwe ya, `/tmp` bụ tmpfs 12 GB nke RAM na-akwado, ọnụ ụzọ nnwapụta ahụ wee mefuo ohere ya niile, nke mere ka package ghara izu ezu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS`        | _(ndabara dị na koodu)_       | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Ogologo oge n'etiti flush (ms) maka writer mmefu/ọnụ ahịa nke a na-achịkọta n'ìgwè. Uru ndị dị ala na-ebelata njikọta odide; uru ndị dị elu na-ebelata esemokwu DB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_MAX_BUFFER_SIZE`          | _(ndabara dị na koodu)_       | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Ọnụọgụ ntinye mmefu kachasị nwere ike ịnọ na buffer tupu a manye flush. Welie ya na deployment nwere QPS dị elu; belata ya mgbe ebe nchekwa nwere oke dị mkpa karịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_FETCH_DEBUG`              | _(atọghị)_                    | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                        | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `"true"` iji wepụta ndekọ debug `[ProxyFetch]` n'ụzọ relay Vercel. Agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara iji zere ikpughe ntụziaka routing.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                    | `false`                       | `src/lib/proxyLogger.ts`                              | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `"true"` ma ọ bụ `"1"` iji tinye IP ndị client/egress na prefix akaụntụ n'ahịrị ndekọ process zuru ezu `[ProxyEgress]`. A na-edobe ya ka ọ GBANYỤỌ na ndabara ka ndekọ process ghara ikpughe IP ma ọ bụ prefix akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG`                          | _(atọghị)_                    | `bin/cli/commands/quota.mjs`                          | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji bipụta nchọpụta oge nke arịrịọ ọ bụla (`[omniroute] GET <path> completed in Nms`) site na iwu quota CLI gaa na stderr.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_PATH`               | _(akpaka)_                    | `scripts/dev/healthcheck.mjs`                         | Path akọwapụtara nke nyocha ahụike container na-enyocha. Mgbe atọghị ya, probe na-enweta ya site na `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`; ịtọ ya ga-eme ka e jiri endpoint nlekota miri emi mee ihe ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG_COMPLETION`               | _(atọghị)_                    | `bin/cli/commands/completion.mjs`                     | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ uru ọ bụla na-abụghị efu iji wepụta nchọpụta `[omniroute completion]` site na path cache mmecha shell CLI (ịgụ/imegharị/ide). Agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara — cache ndị ahụ na-ada na nzuzo ka cache na-efu efu/mebiri emebi ghara imebi mmecha site na tab.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `BATCH_RETRY_DURATION_MS`                  | `86400000` (24h)              | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Ogologo oge kachasị elu maka ịnwalegharị ihe batch ọ bụla (ms). A na-akara ihe ndị gafere oge a dị ka ndị dara ada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_BASE_MS`                    | `5000`                        | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Oge nchere ntọala (ms) maka mmụba oge nchere n'ụdị exponential mgbe a na-anwalegharị ihe batch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_MAX_MS`                     | `3600000` (1h)                | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Oke kachasị (ms) maka mmụba oge nchere n'ụdị exponential n'etiti nnwalegharị ihe batch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `BATCH_MAX_CONCURRENT`                     | `1`                           | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Ọnụọgụ batch kachasị elu a ga-ahazi n'otu oge. Bulie ya iji mee ka ọrụ na-aga ngwa ngwa; debe ya ala iji zere ọtụtụ arịrịọ gafere oke ọnụego.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Tupu a gbanwee ọdụ data na-adịgide adịgide nke dịbu, migration runner na-ebipụta snapshot zuru ezu,
> nke adreesị ọdịnaya na-achịkwa n'okpuru `DATA_DIR/db_backups/`. Mbipụta chọrọ filesystem
> nke na-akwado hard link ndị dị n'otu filesystem, ndị anaghị edegharị faịlụ dịbu, yana file sync na-adịgide adịgide. Ndị ọbịa POSIX chọkwara
> directory sync; na Windows, Node nwere ike ịjụ directory handles, ya mere OmniRoute na-eme flush
> faịlụ e bipụtara ma were directory-entry sync dị ka mgbalị kacha mma.
> Ọ bụrụ na `DATA_DIR` e tinyere enweghị ike ịnye nkwa ndị ahụ, mmalite ga-akwụsị n'ụzọ echekwara tupu etinye
> migration. Bugharịa `DATA_DIR` gaa na volume nwere ikike ndị ahụ; ejila
> `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP` gafere nchekwa migration.

### Ọnọdụ ojiji

| Ọnọdụ ojiji                  | Nhazi                                                                           |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Mmepe mpaghara**           | Hapụ ntọala niile dịka ha dị. DB dị na `~/.omniroute/omniroute.db`.             |
| **Docker**                   | `DATA_DIR=/data` + tinye volume na `/data`.                                     |
| **Ezoro ezo mgbe echekwara** | Tọọ `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY` + debe ndabere nke key ahụ! Ịtụfu ya = ịtụfu data. |
| **CI/Nnwale**                | `DATA_DIR=/tmp/omniroute-test` — ọ bụ nke nwa oge, encryption adịghị mkpa.      |

---

## 3. Netwọkụ & Ọdụ Ụzọ

| Mgbanwe                                     | Ndabara                                     | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PORT`                                      | `20128`                                     | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Ọdụ ụzọ bụ isi maka Dashboard UI na ebe njedebe API (ọnọdụ otu ọdụ ụzọ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`                       | _(efu = mgbọrọgwụ)_                         | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/docker/ensure-docker-base-path.mjs`          | Obere ụzọ URL maka izisa OmniRoute n'azụ reverse proxy (na-edobe Next.js `basePath`; ntụgharị auth na-eburu basePath n'uche). Dịka ọmụmaatụ `/omniroute`. Na Docker, a na-etinye uru ahụ n'oge `docker build` (`ARG OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`); onyonyo mgbọrọgwụ e wurula nwere ike itinye uru runtime dị iche otu ugboro mgbe container na-amalite tupu Next.js ebido. Tọọ `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` ka ọ bụrụ origin ọha nke gụnyere otu obere ụzọ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`           | _(efu = mgbọrọgwụ)_                         | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`                                  | Oyiri `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` nke browser nwere ike ịhụ, nke a na-etinye kpọmkwem n'oge build ka ngosipụta ebe njedebe dashboard gosi `https://host/omniroute/v1` kama `https://host/v1`. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, ọ na-eji `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`. Wughachi mgbe ị gbanwechara ya (Next `basePath` bụ nke oge build).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED`                     | _(edoghị = enweghị ike itinye ya na frame)_ | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/dashboardEmbed.mjs`                    | Nhọrọ iji kwado itinye ibe HTML n'ime iframe. Mgbe edoghị ya, route ọ bụla na-ebute `frame-ancestors 'none'` + `X-Frame-Options: DENY`. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `vscode` iji zisa ibe ndị ahụ (dashboard, login, docs, landing) nwere `frame-ancestors 'self' vscode-webview:` ma na-enweghị `X-Frame-Options`, ka VS Code Simple Browser nwee ike ịrụpụta ha (ọnọdụ `dashboardOpen: "editor"` nke OmniCopilot). Akụkụ API (`/api`, `/v1`, `/v1beta`, `/a2a`, `/healthz`, aliases dị n'ogo mgbọrọgwụ) na-ejigide headers siri ike n'ọnọdụ abụọ ahụ. Naanị `vscode` ka a na-amata — `1`/`true` anaghị akwado ya. Oge build: wughachi mgbe ị gbanwechara ya (`docker build --build-arg DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED=vscode` maka onyonyo; ịtọ ya na nrụnye e wurula enweghị mmetụta). |
| `API_PORT`                                  | _(edoghị)_                                  | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Mgbe edobere ya, ọ na-enye proxy API `/v1/*` na ọdụ ụzọ a dị iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `API_HOST`                                  | `0.0.0.0`                                   | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Adreesị bind maka ọdụ ụzọ API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `DASHBOARD_PORT`                            | _(edoghị)_                                  | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Mgbe edobere ya, ọ na-enye Dashboard UI na ọdụ ụzọ a dị iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNI_MAX_CONCURRENT_CONNECTIONS`           | `0` _(agbanyụrụ)_                           | `src/sse/utils/backpressure.ts`                                          | Na-amachi njikọ nkata ndị na-aga n'otu oge; arịrịọ karịrị oke ahụ na-enweta `503` nwere `Retry-After`. Nọmba zuru ezu dị mma na-akwado nchedo ahụ; mgbe edoghị ya ma ọ bụ ọ bụrụ `0`, ọ na-agbanyụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTANCE_ID`                     | _(edoghị)_                                  | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | ID kwụsiri ike na pụrụ iche maka gateway a mgbe a na-ejikọta instance OmniRoute n'usoro. Ọ na-akwado nyocha loop peer na-abata. Akara ndị ekwe: mkpụrụedemede, ọnụọgụ, `.`, `_`, `:`, na `-`; kachasị mkpụrụedemede 64.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_URLS`                       | _(edobeghị ya)_                             | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`     | URL ntọala OmniRoute ndị e ji rikoma kewaa nke nwere ike ịnata `X-OmniRoute-Peer-Trace`. Naanị URL upstream ndị e tinyere n'ụzọ doro anya na ndepụta nkwenye ga-enweta metadata peer; a naghị emetụ ndị na-eweta ọrụ ndị ọzọ niile aka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_MAX_HOPS`                   | `4`                                         | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | Ọnụọgụ kachasị elu nke instance OmniRoute ndị arịrịọ ejikọrọ gara na mbụ a ga-anabata (`1`-`32`). Instance ndị a na-emegharị ugboro ugboro ma ọ bụ mgbe oke e nyere gwụsịrị ga-eweghachi HTTP `508 Loop Detected`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT`                       | `20130`                                     | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Port e bipụtara n'akụkụ host maka Dashboard n'ụdị mmepụta Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `PROD_API_PORT`                             | `20131`                                     | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Port e bipụtara n'akụkụ host maka API n'ụdị mmepụta Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PORT`                            | _(edobeghị ya)_                             | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Ọ na-ebute ụzọ karịa `PORT` mgbe a na-agba ya n'ime Electron ma ọ bụ wrapper ndị ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `LIVE_WS_PORT`                              | `20129`                                     | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Port maka sava nlekota ozugbo nke WebSocket.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `LIVE_WS_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                                 | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Adreesị bind maka sava WebSocket ozugbo. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0.0.0.0` iji mee ka a nweta ya na LAN (haziekwa `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                   | _(edobeghị ya)_                             | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Origin ndị ọzọ e ji rikoma kewaa nke enyere ikike imepe WebSocket ozugbo. A na-enyerịrị origin dashboard loopback ikike na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_HOSTS`                     | _(edobeghị ya)_                             | `src/server/ws/liveServerAllowList.ts`                                   | Aha host ndị ọzọ e ji rikoma kewaa nke enyere ikike maka origin WebSocket ozugbo. N'adịghị ka `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` (URL origin zuru ezu), ọ na-atụnyere naanị akụkụ host — nke bara uru maka nhazi LAN/Tailscale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_WS_PUBLIC_URL`            | _(edobeghị ya)_                             | `src/hooks/useLiveDashboard.ts`                                          | URL ọha maka WebSocket dashboard ozugbo (n'akụkụ ihe nchọgharị). Tọọ ya mgbe reverse proxy ma ọ bụ Cloudflare Tunnel na-anọ n'ihu sava WS (dịka `wss://ws.my-ai.com/live-ws`); ihe nchọgharị ga-ejikọta ebe ahụ kama `ws://hostname:20132`. A na-ejikwa akụkụ pathname dịka ụzọ nkwalite WebSocket (ndabara: `/live-ws`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_ENABLE_LIVE_WS`                  | `true`                                      | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts` na `scripts/start-ws-server.mjs`           | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` ma ọ bụ `false` iji gbanyụọ sava WebSocket ozugbo (agbanyere ya na ndabara, ma jikọta ya na loopback). Ọ bụ toggle CI/harness nke na-gbanyụ skripụ enyemaka WebSocket ozugbo kwụụrụ onwe ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE`                       | `30`                                        | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/route.ts`                         | Oke ọsọ relay kwa (token, IP), arịrịọ kwa nkeji. Ọ dị na memory, kwa instance. `0` ma ọ bụ uru na-adịghị mma na-gbanyụ ọnụ ụzọ njikwa nke akụkụ IP (oke DB kwa token ka na-emetụta).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `NODE_ENV`                                  | `production`                                | isi Next.js                                                              | Na-achịkwa oke ndekọ, caching, ngosipụta nkọwa njehie, na nkwalite Next.js.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK`                   | `1` (Turbopack — ndabara koodu)             | `package.json` / Next.js 16                                              | Turbopack bụ bundler ndabara maka `npm run dev` na `npm run build` (build dị ngwa ugboro 2-3 dịka benchmark gosiri). Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji laghachi na webpack na Windows, mgbe e nwere nsogbu ndakọrịta native binding / bundler, **ma ọ bụ na kọmputa RAM ha pere mpe** — a maara n'akụkụ upstream na build production nke Turbopack n'ahịrị ụdị Next.js a (16.2.x) na-eru oke ojiji ebe nchekwa dị elu karịa webpack mgbe module graph buru ibu (ndozi memory-eviction nke Turbopack na Next 16.3 akwụsibeghị ike); iji webpack dịka ndabere na-eru oke ebe nchekwa dị ala nke ukwuu. Lee #6409.                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`             | _(edobeghị)_                                | `src/lib/db/core.ts` / `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`                       | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji wụfee nyocha ahụike nke iguzosi ike n'ezi ihe SQLite mgbe usoro na-amalite. Ọ bara uru maka mmalite dị ngwa na database buru ibu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `NOTIFY_SOCKET`                             | _(edobeghị)_                                | systemd (protokọl sd_notify)                                             | systemd na-edobe ya mgbe process na-agba n'okpuru service unit nwere njikọta sd_notify; OmniRoute na-agụ ya (lee `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`) iji zipụ ọkwa READY/WATCHDOG. Onye ọrụ ekwesịghị idobe ya ma ọlị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`               | _(edobeghị)_                                | `scripts/dev/systemd-notify.mjs`                                         | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji gbanyụọ systemd sd_notify (Type=notify / WatchdogSec=) ọbụlagodi mgbe ọ na-agba n'okpuru systemd unit. Notifier ahụ anaghị eme ihe ọ bụla n'èzí systemd n'agbanyeghị ọnọdụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK_INTERVAL`          | `300000`                                    | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts` | Ogologo oge (ms) n'etiti nyocha nke scheduler nyocha ahụike credential na-arụ n'azụ. Nke kacha nta: 10000 (10s).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL`               | `300000`                                    | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/cache.ts`     | TTL (ms) maka ọnọdụ ahụike credential e debere na cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK` | `false`                                     | `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts`                                  | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` ma ọ bụ `true` iji gbanyụọ nnwale njikọ provider nke a na-eme kwa oge n'azụ. A na-ewepụ search providers (SEARCH_VALIDATOR_CONFIGS dị na `src/lib/providers/validation/searchProviders.ts`, dịka `tavily-search`) mgbe niile na nyocha ahụ — "validation" ha bụ ezigbo query upstream a na-akwụ ụgwọ maka ya, ya mere a naghị eji timer enyocha ahụike ha ma ọlị (#9970).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `HOST`                                      | `0.0.0.0`                                   | `scripts/dev/run-next.mjs`                                               | Adreesị bind maka server dev/start nke Next.js. Ọ na-anọchi `0.0.0.0` ndabara mgbe edobere ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `HOSTNAME`                                  | `127.0.0.1`                                 | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`                                    | Adreesị bind nke Playwright runner na-eji mgbe ọ na-ebido Next.js. Ndabara ya bụ `127.0.0.1` maka ule hermetic. **Ejila ya maka `omniroute serve`** — jiri `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST` kama (shell POSIX na-edobe `HOSTNAME` n'onwe ya ka ọ bụrụ aha kọmputa ahụ; `.env` enweghị ike dochie ya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST`                     | `0.0.0.0`                                   | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                             | Adreesị bind maka `omniroute serve`. Ọ na-ezere nkukota ya na variable `HOSTNAME` nke shell POSIX (bash/zsh na-edobe ya mgbe niile ka ọ bụrụ aha kọmputa ahụ). Ọ na-alaghachi na `0.0.0.0` mgbe edobeghị ya. (#6194)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

### Ụdị Port

```
┌──────────────────────────── Otu Pọtụ (ndabara) ────────────────────────────┐
│  PORT=20128                                                                 │
│  → Ogwe njikwa: http://localhost:20128                                      │
│  → API:         http://localhost:20128/v1/chat/completions                  │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌──────────────────────────── Pọtụ Ndị E Kewara ─────────────────────────────┐
│  DASHBOARD_PORT=20128                                                       │
│  API_PORT=20129                                                             │
│  API_HOST=0.0.0.0                                                           │
│  → Ogwe njikwa: http://localhost:20128                                      │
│  → API:         http://0.0.0.0:20129/v1/chat/completions                   │
│  Ojiji: Mee ka LAN nweta API ma machibido Ogwe njikwa na localhost.         │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌──────────────────────────── Mmepụta Docker ────────────────────────────────┐
│  PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT=443   PROD_API_PORT=8443                              │
│  → Na-ejikọta pọtụ kọntena na pọtụ host na docker-compose.prod.yml.        │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 4. Nchekwa & Nnwale Njirimara
