| Mgbanwe                                     | Ndabara                                                                                           | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MACHINE_ID_SALT`                           | `endpoint-proxy-salt`                                                                             | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Salt a na-ejikọta na njirimara ngwaike iji mepụta akara mkpịsị aka igwe. Gbanwee ya maka nrụnye ọ bụla iji kewapụ ha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SALT`                        | _(edoghị = salt enweghị usoro maka nrụnye ọ bụla, echekwara na `<DATA_DIR>/cli-token-salt.json`)_ | `src/lib/machineToken.ts`                                | Salt HMAC maka iwepụta token nnwale njirimara CLI mpaghara. Ịtọ uru a na-eme ka token CLI niile dị n'igwe ahụ gbanwee, ọ na-ebukwa ụzọ mgbe niile karịa salt echekwara. Lee `docs/security/CLI_TOKEN.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE`                        | `false`                                                                                           | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Na-etinye ọkọlọtọ `Secure` na kuki nnọkọ. **Ọ ga-abụrịrị `true`** mgbe a na-agba ya n'azụ HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `REQUIRE_API_KEY`                           | `false`                                                                                           | Middleware API                                           | Mgbe ọ bụ `true`, arịrịọ proxy `/v1/*` niile ga-etinyerịrị igodo API ziri ezi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL`                      | `false`                                                                                           | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`         | Na-enye ohere igosipụta uru igodo API zuru ezu na UI Dashboard. Enwere ike ịhazi ya site na Dashboard Feature Flags; ọ bụ ihe ize ndụ nchekwa na instance ndị ọtụtụ mmadụ na-eji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `NO_LOG_API_KEY_IDS`                        | _(gụgbọọrọ)_                                                                                      | `src/lib/compliance/index.ts`                            | ID igodo API ndị e ji rikoma kewaa, nke na-eme ka a ghara idekọ arịrịọ ha (nrubeisi GDPR).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `DEFAULT_RATE_LIMIT_PER_DAY`                | _(edoghị = enweghị oke)_                                                                          | `src/shared/utils/apiKeyPolicy.ts`                       | Oke ndabere nke arịrịọ kwa ụbọchị a na-etinye na igodo API ndị kọlụm `rate_limits` ha bụ null. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya ma ọ bụ na ọ tọgbọọrọ: enweghị oke e tinyere na nzuzo (#2289, #11017). `0` pụtakwara otu ihe ahụ (enweghị oke). Integer dị mma N na-enye N/ụbọchị, 5N/izu, 20N/ọnwa. Uru na-adịghị tọgbọọrọ ma ahaziri ya n'ụzọ na-ezighị ezi ga-alaghachi n'usoro ochie nke 1000/ụbọchị, 5000/izu, 20000/ọnwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES`                       | `10485760` (10 MB)                                                                                | `src/shared/middleware/bodySizeGuard.ts`                 | Ogo kachasị nke body arịrịọ a na-anabata. Ọ na-ajụ payload ndị gafere oke a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`           | `262144` (256 KB)                                                                                 | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Body arịrịọ n'ezie ndị ruru ma ọ bụ gafere oke a na-enweta lease nnabata heavyweight nke atomic na process mpaghara tupu a nyochaa JSON (ụzọ BYTE, gụnyere `POST /v1/responses`). Ọ na-eji otu ụzọ mgbapụ [#10437](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/pull/10437) nke ohere fọdụrụ dị mma dịka nke nwere nhazi dị arọ; `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / [#10110](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/10110) ka na-amachi ya ka [#7849](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/7849) ghara ịlaghachi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_BODY_BYTES`        | `52428800` (50 MB)                                                                                | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Oke siri ike nke ụzọ nkata, nke a na-amanye megide byte ndị a gụrụ n'oge nnabata nwere oke, gụnyere arịrịọ ndị `Content-Length` ha adịghị, na-ezighi ezi, ma ọ bụ na-enye ozi ụgha; nke gafere oke ga-enweta `413`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT`        | _(edobeghị — enweghị oke ọnụọgụ arịrịọ)_                                                          | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | **#503-fanout:** oke ỌNỤỌGỤ arịrịọ ochie a na-amalite ịrụ ọrụ naanị mgbe edobere ya kpọmkwem. Mgbe edoghị ya (ndabara), nnabata nkata dị arọ na-adabere kama na `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` — mmefu BYTE a na-emepụta na-akpaghị aka, nke a na-ahazi site n'oke ebe nchekwa n'ezie nke usoro ahụ n'ime **otu usoro** (otu V8 heap). Arịrịọ `/v1/responses` abụọ na-adakọta, nke nke ọ bụla nwere ihe dịka token 750k, na-eme ka heap ihe dịka 12 Gi kwụsị (#7849) — nke a bụ ịdọ aka ná ntị gbasara **mmefu ebe nchekwa**, ọ bụghị oke ngwaahịa siri ike nke 2. Usoro dị mma (heap dị n'okpuru oke nha ntọhapụ) NWERE IKE ịnabata ọtụtụ `/v1/responses` ogologo n'otu oge site na `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM`. Ọtụtụ iri ndị ahịa SSE ogologo (40–50) dabere na heap + `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / #10110, ọ bụghị “oke 2”. Ịkwalite nke a n'amaghị ama iji “ebe nchekwa host ahụ niile” ga-eweghachite #7849. Mee ka ọnụọgụ **heap** bawanye site na **N `DATA_DIR` nọọrọ onwe ha** (#11024); ejila `replicas>1` n'otu faịlụ SQLite. |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES`         | _(a na-emepụta na-akpaghị aka)_                                                                   | `src/shared/middleware/admissionBudget.ts`               | **#503-fanout:** mgbanwe dochie anya mmefu byte mbubata a na-emepụta na-akpaghị aka (25% nke oke ebe nchekwa V8/cgroup nke pere mpe, kewara site na mmụba nwa oge 8x). A na-amachi ụkpụrụ emepụtara na nke edobere kpọmkwem n'etiti 8 MiB–2 GiB. Body buru ibu karịa mmefu dị irè na-ada ozugbo na `413 body_exceeds_budget`; asọmpi n'etiti body ndị a pụrụ ijere n'otu n'otu ka bụ `503` a pụrụ ịnwale ọzọ. Ndị ahịa SSE ogologo 40–50 na-arụ ọrụ n'otu oge dabere na mmefu a + heap, ọ bụghị “oke 2” siri ike. Gụọ `chatAdmission.maxInflightBytes` / `budgetSource` / `pressureSeverity` na `/api/monitoring/health` tupu imezigharị ntọala.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEAP_SHED_RATIO`  | `0.75`                                                                                            | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Nha ntọhapụ n'ihi nrụgide heap (`heapUsed / heap_size_limit`) maka nnabata BYTE na STRUCTURE dị arọ (#10183, #10268, #10437). A na-ahapụ arịrịọ dị arọ na-arụ n'otu oge nke gafere `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` naanị site na `503` a pụrụ ịnwale ọzọ mgbe heap DỊKWA na nha a ma ọ bụ karịa ya; mgbe heap dị mma, a na-anabata ya kama site na ohere mgbakwunye nke heap dị mma.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM` | `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` (ndabara `1`)                                                | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ikike mgbakwunye nwere oke maka ụzọ ọsọ ọsọ nke heap dị mma (#10437) na **ma** STRUCTURE **ma** BYTE (`admitChatRequest`, gụnyere body ≥ `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`). Enweghị oke a, arịrịọ ọ bụla nwere ọrụ buru ibu mana heap ya dị mma na-agafe nnabata n'enweghị oke elu. Ozugbo ọnụọgụ lease ndị a na-arụ n'otu oge site n'ụzọ heap dị mma ruru nke a, arịrịọ ndị ọzọ nwere ọrụ buru ibu ga-adaba n'otu ụzọ echere-nwere-oke/ntọhapụ ahụ a na-eji mgbe heap nọ n'okpuru ezigbo nrụgide. `0` na-agbanyụ ngafe ahụ kpamkpam.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_MESSAGE_COUNT`        | `200`                                                                                             | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ọnụọgụ ozi nke na-ekewa arịrịọ nkata dịka nke dị arọ ọbụna mgbe body ya dị n'okpuru oke byte.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_TOOL_COUNT`           | `64`                                                                                              | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ọnụọgụ tool nke na-ekewa arịrịọ nkata dịka nke dị arọ ọbụna mgbe body ya dị n'okpuru oke byte.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_ESTIMATED_TOKENS`     | `32000`                                                                                           | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Atụmatụ token nke nha string e ji nlezianya mee, nke na-ekewa arịrịọ dịka nke dị arọ; nke a bụ ihe nnọchi anya ọnụ ahịa nnabata, ọ bụghị nhazi token maka ịgba ụgwọ nke provider.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_MESSAGES`          | `0` (agbanyụrụ)                                                                                   | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Oke akụkọ nkata nhọrọ nke a ga-ahọrọ ịgbanye. Agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara: ọnụọgụ ozi bụ iwu deployment, ọ bụghị njirimara zuru ụwa ọnụ nke arịrịọ, ma itinye oke ebe a na-ajụ mkparịta ụka site na `413` ikpeazụ tupu pipeline mkpakọ enwee ike ime ka e nwee ike ijere ha ozi. `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` na ntọhapụ n'ihi nrụgide heap na-egbochi uto heap. Debe ụkpụrụ dị mma karịrị efu na deployment nwere oke ebe nchekwa nke chọrọ oke elu siri ike; ihe gafere oke ga-enwetazi `413` ahaziri nke chọrọ mkpakọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_NONSTREAMING_RESPONSE_BYTES` | `67108864` (64 MB)                                                                                | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore/nonStreamingResponseBody.ts` | Oke siri ike maka nzaghachi upstream na-abụghị streaming nke a na-etinye kpamkpam n'ime ebe nchekwa. Mgbe ọ gafere nke a, a na-akagbu upstream reader, arịrịọ ahụ adaa ngwa ngwa kama ịga n'ihu na-eme ka string na-enweghị oke too ruo mgbe heap gwụsịrị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORWARDING_HEADER_BUDGET_BYTES`  | `768`                                                                                             | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore/responseHeaders.ts`          | Ọnụọgụ wire byte kachasị elu a na-ebufe site na header nzaghachi upstream. Mgbe a gafere mmefu ahụ, a na-ewepụ header ndị nwere mkpa dị ala (dịka ọmụmaatụ, `x-codex-*`, `x-oai-request-id`) iji nọrọ n'ime oke header reverse-proxy a na-ahụkarị. Debe ya elu karịa iji bufee metadata upstream karịa, mana nke ahụ ga-eme ka nha header nzaghachi bawanye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CORS_ORIGIN`                               | _(edobeghị)_                                                                                      | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Ndepụta ikike CORS nke otu origin nke ochie. Maka ntinye ọhụrụ, họrọ `CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`. CORS bụ naanị maka ndị ahịa API ihe nchọgharị sitere na origin dị iche; kama nke ahụ, ide ihe na dashboard nke agbasiri ike na-eji arịrịọ same-origin tinyere nchedo CSRF ejikọtara na session.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                      | _(edobeghị)_                                                                                      | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Ndepụta ikike CORS e ji rikoma kewaa. A naghị eziga wildcard ọ bụla ma ọ bụrụ na ahazighị `CORS_ALLOW_ALL=true` n'ụzọ doro anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CORS_ALLOW_ALL`                            | `false`                                                                                           | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Ụzọ mgbapụ maka mmepe naanị nke na-emegharị `Origin` ihe nchọgharị ọ bụla. Emegbula ya na ntinye ndị a na-ekekọrịta ma ọ bụ nke mmepụta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED`               | `true`                                                                                            | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuard.ts`                 | Gbochie oku provider ndị na-elekwasị anya na oke adreesị IP private/loopback/link-local. Gbanyụọ ya naanị na gburugburu ule ndị e kewapụrụ iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`     | `false`                                                                                           | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuard.ts`                 | Kwe ka URL provider rụtụ aka na netwọkụ private/local (localhost, 192.168.x.x, 10.x.x.x, wdg.). **ACHỌRỌ ya maka provider ndị a na-akwado n'onwe** (LM Studio, Ollama, vLLM, Llamafile, Triton, SearXNG). Mgbe ọ bụ `false`, dashboard na-ajụ nkwado URL local.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS`       | `true`                                                                                            | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuard.ts`                 | Kwe ka ịgbakwunye/ịkwado provider na adreesị local/private (127.0.0.1, localhost, LAN, oke private) — nke a metụtara naanị ụzọ nkwado provider. **Ndabara bụ `true`** (local-first); tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji manye mgbochi siri ike nke na-anabata naanị nke ọha. A na-anọgide na-egbochi endpoint metadata igwe ojii (169.254.169.254, metadata.google.internal) n'agbanyeghị nke ahụ. (#5066)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`               | `false`                                                                                           | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/audioProviderNodes.ts`           | Kwe ka route `/v1/audio/*` (ntụgharị ọdịyo gaa na ederede, okwu, ntụgharị asụsụ) jiri node provider dakọtara na OpenAI nke a na-akwado n'èzí localhost. A gbanyụrụ ya na ndabara — iziga ọdịyo na host remote na-agbanwe njirimara egress, ya mere ọ ga-abụrịrị mkpebi onye na-ahụ maka sistemụ mere n'ụzọ doro anya. A na-anabata node loopback/private (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) mgbe niile, nke a anaghịkwa emetụta ha. (#3963)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`     | `false`                                                                                           | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Mgbe agbanyere OIDC, gbanyụọ nbanye site na paswọọdụ ka ndị ọrụ nwee ike igosipụta njirimara naanị site na OIDC Single Sign-On. A na-anabatakwa alias nkịtị `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`; Dashboard Feature Flag nwere otu key ahụ na-ebute ụzọ. (#10889)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`               | `false`                                                                                           | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Alias nkịtị nke `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN` (#10889).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

### Ndepụta Nlele Mgbasikwu Nchedo

```bash
# Nchedo kacha nta maka mmepụta:
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true        # Chọrọ HTTPS
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true           # Gosipụta njirimara maka oku proxy niile
ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL=false     # Ekpughela key na UI ma ọlị
CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=https://your.domain.com
MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES=5242880    # Oke 5 MB
```

---

## 5. Nhichapụ Ihe Ntinye & Nchedo PII

OmniRoute na-enye nchedo nwere ọkwa abụọ: nyocha ntụnye ọjọọ n'akụkụ arịrịọ na iwepụ PII n'akụkụ nzaghachi.

> **⚠️ Oke ya:** Usoro nchedo ndị a bụ nchọpụta _heuristic nke a na-agba mbọ ime nke ọma_, ọ bụghị firewall zuru oke megide ntụnye ọjọọ na prompt ma ọ bụ sistemụ PII DLP. Ha nwere ike iwepụta nchọpụta ụgha (dịka ịkọpụta prompt persona/RPG na-enweghị nsogbu dịka nke ọjọọ) na enweghị nchọpụta mgbe ihe ọjọọ dị (dịka leetspeak, oghere dị iche iche, na usoro na-abụghị Bekee). Naanị ha ezughị maka irube isi n'ụkpụrụ. Hazie mode ndị ahụ ma nwalee ha megide traffic gị tupu ịdabere na ha.

### N'akụkụ Arịrịọ: Nchedo Megide Prompt Injection

| Variable                          | Nke Ndabara  | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                |
| --------------------------------- | ------------ | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED`         | `true`       | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Na-eme ka nyocha ozi ndị na-abata maka usoro prompt injection rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                               |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE`            | `warn`       | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Iwu injection: `warn` = dekọọ naanị, `block` = jụ arịrịọ site na 400. `redact` ochie **anaghị** ewepụ ederede injection; jiri `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED` maka idegharị PII dị n'arịrịọ. |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_MODE`            | _(ahazighị)_ | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Aha nnọchi ochie maka `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE` — otu omume ahụ.                                                                                                                        |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | `high`       | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Ogo ihe ize ndụ kacha nta nke `MODE=block` ga-ajụ: `high` (ndabara), `medium`, ma ọ bụ `low`. A na-elele usoro `medium` naanị ma ọ bụrụ na e belataghị ya.                           |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | _(ahazighị)_ | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Aha nnọchi ochie maka `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` — otu omume ahụ.                                                                                                             |
| `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`           | `false`      | `src/lib/guardrails/piiMasker.ts`        | Mgbe ọ bụ `true`, zoo PII n'arịrịọ ndị na-abata (ọ naghị adabere na mode injection).                                                                                                 |
| `CREDENTIAL_REDACTION_ENABLED`    | `false`      | `src/lib/guardrails/credentialMasker.ts` | Zoo usoro API-key / secret-token ndị a ma ama na payload arịrịọ/nzaghachi. A ga-ahọrọ ya ka ọ rụọ ọrụ; ọ na-eme dịka `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`.                                        |

### N'akụkụ Nzaghachi: Ihe Nhichapụ PII

| Variable                         | Nke Ndabara | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite         | Nkọwa                                                                        |
| -------------------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION`      | `false`     | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | Nyochaa nzaghachi LLM maka PII gbapụrụ tupu iziga ya nye client.             |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION_MODE` | `redact`    | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | `redact` = zoo PII, `warn` = dekọọ naanị, `block` = tụfuo nzaghachi ahụ dum. |

### Ihe Nhichapụ Context nke Tokenized-Route na VS Code

| Variable                            | Nke Ndabara | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VSCODE_SANITIZE_CONTEXT` | `1`         | `src/app/api/v1/vscode/contextSanitizer.ts` | Na-ewepụ context implicit nke active-editor (`editorContext`, `activeEditor`, `currentFile`, `selection`, `openTabs`…) n'arịrịọ `/v1/vscode/[token]/*` ma zoo ọdịnaya faịlụ nwere mmetụta dị elu ndị e tinyere n'ụzọ doro anya. Ọ dị nchebe na ndabara; tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya. |

### Ọnọdụ Ojiji

| Ọnọdụ Ojiji                    | Nhazi                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Irube isi n'ụkpụrụ ụlọ ọrụ** | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=block`, `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED=true`, `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION=true` (igbochi injection + izochi PII dị n'arịrịọ/nzaghachi; mode ndị ahụ anaghị adabere ibe ha) |
| **Nlekota naanị**              | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=warn` — na-edekọ mana ọ naghị egbochi mgbe ọ bụla                                                                                                                 |
| **Ojiji nkeonwe**              | Hapụ ha niile ka ha ghara ịrụ ọrụ — enweghị overhead ọ bụla                                                                                                                                                             |

---

## 6. Iwu Ngwaọrụ & Nhọrọ Ụzọ

| Mgbanwe                                                     | Ndabara                      | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                              | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `TOOL_POLICY_MODE`                                          | `disabled`                   | `src/lib/toolPolicy.ts`                    | Na-achịkwa ohere LLM nwere iji kpọọ ngwaọrụ/ọrụ. `allowlist` = naanị ngwaọrụ ndị edepụtara, `denylist` = ha niile ma e wezụga ndị edepụtara, `disabled` = enweghị mmachi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_PATH`                              | `./config/payloadRules.json` | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`        | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ JSON nke iwu mgbanwe payload (mmegharị upstream ndị dabere na model/protocol).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_RELOAD_MS`                         | `5000`                       | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`        | Oge etiti (ms) maka ibugharị faịlụ iwu payload ọzọ ozugbo mgbanwe mere. Nke kacha nta bụ `1000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PREFER_CLAUDE_CODE_FOR_UNPREFIXED_CLAUDE_MODELS` | `false`                      | `open-sse/services/model.ts`               | Nhọrọ mmadụ ga-eji aka ya mee: zipụ ID model `claude-*` ndị na-enweghị prefix sitere na ndị ahịa Claude Code site na akaụntụ OAuth Claude Code kama ịchọ prefix onye na-eweta ọrụ. Prefix onye na-eweta ọrụ e depụtara kpọmkwem ka na-ebute ụzọ. A pụkwara ịhazi ya site na mgba ọkụ dashboard dị na peeji onye na-eweta Claude.                                                                       |
| `COMBO_CONCURRENCY_PER_MODEL`                               | `3`                          | `open-sse/services/comboConfig.ts`         | Oke concurrency maka model ọ bụla n'ime combo round-robin (#9100). E debere semaphore combo round-robin n'ụzọ siri ike na arịrịọ 3 na-aga n'otu oge maka model ọ bụla, na-enweghị ụzọ ịgbanwe ya, nke mere ka okporo ụzọ nwere concurrency dị elu kwụ n'ahịrị n'azụ oke ahụ. A na-enyocha ya ka ọ bụrụ `>= 1`, ma na-amachi ya ka ọ bụrụ `<= 32`.                                                      |
| `DISABLE_CONTEXT_WINDOW_CHECKS`                             | `false`                      | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`            | Nhọrọ dị ize ndụ mmadụ ga-eji aka ya mee nke na-awụpụ nyocha mpaghara OmniRoute maka context-window / max-input-token n'arịrịọ kpọmkwem nke otu model. Ndị na-eweta ọrụ upstream ka na-amanye oke ha n'ezie; mkpakọ prompt na oke output-token nke model n'onwe ya ka na-arụ ọrụ. Usoro ibute ụzọ dị irè bụ Feature Flags DB override > environment variable > ndabara; achọghị ịmalitegharịa sistemụ. |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CONVERSATION_TRACKING`                   | _(edoghị ya)_                | `open-sse/services/conversationTracker.ts` | Tọọ `1` iji kwụsị ịnakọta akụkọ mkparịta ụka. `resolveConversationId()` na-eweghachi nsonaazụ anaghị esochi tupu ọ gụọ SQLite ma ọ bụ nyochaa akụkọ ozi, nke a metụtakwara ID session ndị ahịa nyere. Nhazi routing-session agbanweghị, a naghịkwa ehichapụ ndekọ ndị dị adị. Nke a bụ maka nrụnye ndị anaghị eji nlele mkparịta ụka dashboard ma chọọ ka tebụl turn kwụsị ito.                        |

---

## 7. URL na Mmekọrịta Ígwé Ojii

| Mgbanwe                                  | Ndabara                                         | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                                  | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `BASE_URL`                               | `http://localhost:20128`                        | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                         | URL dị n'akụkụ sava nke ọrụ mmekọrịta dị n'ime ga-eji kpọọ `/api/sync/cloud`. Debe nke a ka ọ bụrụ URL loopback/container ọbụlagodi mgbe a na-eji proxy eme ka ngwa ahụ dị n'ihu ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLOUD_URL`                              | _(efu)_                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                         | URL endpoint nke relay ígwé ojii (njirimara akwụmụgwọ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CLOUD_SYNC_TIMEOUT_MS`                  | `12000`                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                         | Oge HTTP ga-echere tupu ọ kwụsị arịrịọ mmekọrịta ígwé ojii.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_PROFILE`                | `full`                                          | Nhazi build nke Webpack                        | Profaịlụ oge build (tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `minimal` iji wepụ modul nwere ikike pụrụ iche kpamkpam na bundle).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`               | _mmepụta standalone nke .build/_                | `scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`        | Ndochi oge build maka ndekọ mmepụta standalone nke usoro colocation mgbe build gasịrị na-eji. Ọ bụghị ntọala runtime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET`            | _(efu)_                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                         | Ihe nzuzo e kere òkè nke a na-eji enyocha mbinye aka HMAC-SHA256 nke nzaghachi Mmekọrịta Ígwé Ojii.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRETS`           | `false`                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                         | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji kwe ka endpoint Mmekọrịta Ígwé Ojii dochie nzere mpaghara. Ndabara bụ `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_ENFORCE_SIGNATURE` | `false`                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                         | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji jụ nzaghachi Mmekọrịta Ígwé Ojii na-enweghị mbinye aka mgbe a hazịghị ihe nzuzo mpaghara (#13679). A na-enyocha mbinye aka ọ bụla dị adị mgbe niile — a na-ajụkwa ya mgbe niile ma ọ bụrụ na edoghị `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET` — n'agbanyeghị ọkọlọtọ a. Na v3.9, ndabara ga-agbanwe ka a manye ya.                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ZED_IMPORT_LEGACY_ONE_STEP`   | `false`                                         | `src/app/api/providers/zed/import/route.ts`    | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji laghachi n'omume otu nzọụkwụ nke v3.8.5, "bubata ihe niile", na-enweghị nkwenye onye ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`                   | `http://localhost:20128`                        | OAuth, Dashboard, mmekọrịta                    | URL ọha maka OAuth redirect_uri, njikọ Dashboard, na URL ọha ndị emepụtara. Tọọ nke a ka ọ bụrụ URL ọha kwụsiri ike mgbe callback OAuth ma ọ bụ njikọ nchọgharị emepụtara ga-eji host reverse-proxy ọkọlọtọ.                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUD_URL`                  | _(efu)_                                         | Akụkụ klayịnt                                  | Oyiri `CLOUD_URL` dị n'akụkụ klayịnt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL`                    | _(edobeghị)_                                    | `src/shared/services/cloudSyncScheduler.ts`    | Nhọrọ ndabere ochie maka `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`              | _(edobeghị)_                                    | Onye na-achọpụta ebe mmalite ọha, URL onyonyo  | Ebe mmalite OmniRoute nke ihe nchọgharị na-ahụ nke nwere mkpa kachasị elu, nke a na-eji emepụta URL ọha na ịkwado ebe mmalite ihe nchọgharị na-abụghị dashboard. Tọọ nke a mgbe OpenWebUI ma ọ bụ relay ọzọ na-eji URL ime nweta OmniRoute, mana ihe nchọgharị onye ọrụ ga-esi na LAN, tunnel, ma ọ bụ ebe mmalite ọha budata mgbasa ozi emepụtara. Etinyekwala **`/v1`**.               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL`        | _(edobeghị)_                                    | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts` | URL zuru oke nke manifest plugin onye na-eweta, nke a na-akpọsa nye ndị ahịa sidecar. Mgbe edoghị ya, OmniRoute na-ewepụta `/api/v1/provider-plugin-manifest` site na ebe arịrịọ si malite ma ọ bụ HOST/PORT.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_PROTOCOL`              | `http`                                          | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts` | Protocol a na-eji mgbe a na-ewepụta URL manifest plugin onye na-eweta site na HOST/PORT na-enweghị ebe arịrịọ si malite. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `https` n'azụ proxy ọha na-akwụsị TLS mgbe edoghị `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL` kpọmkwem.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY`                  | _(edobeghị)_                                    | `src/server/origin/publicOrigin.ts`            | Ụdị ntụkwasị obi nhọrọ maka header ebe mmalite ọha ezipụgoro n'ihu. Edoghị ya = atụkwasịla `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` obi maka mkpebi nchekwa. `true` / `loopback` na-atụkwasị host/proto ezipụgoro n'ihu obi naanị mgbe o si na proxy loopback e tinyere token. `private` / `lan` na-atụkwasịkwa ndị proxy nọ na LAN nzuzo obi. N'ebe mmepụta, họrọ `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` kpọmkwem. |
| `KIE_CALLBACK_URL`                       | _(edobeghị)_                                    | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                    | URL callback ọha maka ọrụ kie.ai na-arụ n'ụzọ asynchronous. Ntọgharị nwere mkpa kachasị elu tupu `OMNIROUTE_KIE_CALLBACK_URL` na `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_KIE_CALLBACK_URL`             | _(edobeghị)_                                    | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                    | Ụzọ ọzọ e si ede `KIE_CALLBACK_URL`. A na-eji ya dịka ndabere mgbe edoghị variable bụ isi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_URL`                   | _(edobeghị)_                                    | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                    | Ebe mmalite ọha a na-eji achịkọta URL callback asynchronous. Nhọrọ ndabere nwere mkpa kacha ala maka callback kie.ai; a na-ejikwa ya dịka URL ọha izugbe maka relay ndị ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CROF_USAGE_URL`               | `https://crof.ai/usage_api/`                    | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                   | Endpoint nchọpụta oke CrofAI nke ibe Usage na-eji. Dochie ya maka relay / fixture nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_OPENCODE_QUOTA_URL`           | `https://opencode.ai/zen/go/v1/usage`           | `open-sse/services/opencodeQuotaFetcher.ts`    | Endpoint ojiji OpenCode Go gọọmentị nke API key na-akwado njirimara, nke ibe Usage na-eji. Dochie ya maka relay / fixture nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS`        | `true`                                          | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`               | Mepụta header njirimara OpenCode CLI (User-Agent, x-opencode-client/project, UUID arịrịọ/session) na arịrịọ opencode-go/zen upstream nke onye ahịa ezipụghị, ka Cloudflare na egress VPS nwee ike ịnabata ha (#6210/#5997). Ọ na-agbanye na ndabara kemgbe #10571; gbanyụọ ya site na `false`/`0`/`no`/`off`.                                                                            |
| `OPENCODE_USER_AGENT`                    | `opencode`                                      | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`               | User-Agent ndabara a na-eji mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` gbanyere ma edoghị ntọgharị `<PROVIDER>_USER_AGENT` maka onye na-eweta ọ bụla. A na-etinye ya naanị na executor opencode.                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OPENCODE_CLIENT`                        | `desktop`                                       | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`               | Uru maka header `x-opencode-client` emepụtara mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` gbanyere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OPENCODE_PROJECT`                       | `global`                                        | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`               | Uru maka nkụnyeisi `x-opencode-project` e mepụtara mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` dị ọkụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_URL`       | `https://ollama.com/settings`                   | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                   | URL ntọala Ollama Cloud e ji enweta ozi oke ojiji. Gbanwee ya maka relays / test fixtures.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`                    | _(edobeghị)_                                    | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                   | Kuki Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session` e ji enweta ozi oke ojiji na ibe ntọala. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; mgbe ahaziri ọtụtụ akaụntụ, họrọ mpaghara Dashboard nke njikọ ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_COOKIE`              | _(edobeghị)_                                    | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                   | Env var ọzọ maka kuki Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session`. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; mgbe ahaziri ọtụtụ akaụntụ, họrọ mpaghara Dashboard nke njikọ ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`          | _(edobeghị)_                                    | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                   | Env var ọzọ maka kuki Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session` nke a na-eji tupu aliases ndị dị mkpụmkpụ. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; mgbe ahaziri ọtụtụ akaụntụ, họrọ mpaghara Dashboard nke njikọ ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEWHISPERER_BASE_URL`       | `https://codewhisperer.us-east-1.amazonaws.com` | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                   | Endpoint oke ojiji CodeWhisperer (AWS Kiro). Gbanwee ya maka relays / test fixtures.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Mgbe ị na-ebunye ya n'azụ reverse proxy (nginx, Caddy), tọọ `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` ka ọ bụrụ URL ọha kwụsiri ike nke gị (dịka ọmụmaatụ, `https://omniroute.example.com`) mgbe OAuth callbacks ma ọ bụ njikọ ọha e mepụtara ga-eji hostname ahụ. E wezụga nke a, OAuth callbacks nwere ike ịda n'ihi na redirect_uri agaghị adaba, njikọ ọha e mepụtara nwekwara ike ịtụ aka na internal container origin.
>
> Debe `BASE_URL` dị ka URL internal loopback/container maka ọrụ server-to-server. Ejila `Origin` nke ihe nchọgharị ma ọ bụ hostname ọha maka internal self-fetches ndị nwere credential.
>
> Ndekọ dashboard enwetara ikike anaghị achọ static public base URL: dashboard na-eziga unsafe requests nke same-origin yana CSRF token ejikọrọ na session. OmniRoute ka na-achịkọta nkwado public-origin n'otu ebe maka njikọ ihe nchọgharị ndị na-abụghị dashboard: a na-ebu ụzọ tụkwasị env vars URL ọha akọwapụtara obi; a na-eleghara raw `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` headers anya ma ọ bụrụ na agbanyereghị `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY` ma jirikwa token kaa immediate proxy peer akara dị ka nke a tụkwasịrị obi. Ejila ntọala CORS dozie same-origin dashboard requests; CORS bụ naanị maka cross-origin browser clients.

---

## 8. Proxy Na-apụ Apụ

Ziga oku ndị na-eweta LLM dị n'elu site na proxy HTTP ma ọ bụ SOCKS5 maka njikwa okporo ụzọ na-apụ apụ, nhazi ụzọ dabere na mpaghara, ma ọ bụ izochi IP.

| Variable                                 | Ndabara      | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                            | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ---------------------------------------- | ------------ | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`                    | `true`       | `open-sse/executors`                         | Mee ka onye nnọchi anya proxy SOCKS5 rụọ ọrụ maka oku ndị na-aga n'elu. Jiri `false` họrọ ịghara iji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`        | `true`       | Akụkụ onye ahịa                              | Nghọta dị n'akụkụ onye ahịa banyere ịdị adị nke SOCKS5.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`             | `false`      | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`           | Ọkọlọtọ atụmatụ a na-ahọrọ iji (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ntọala dochie nke DB dashboard ga-emeri). Otu proxy na ntụgharị nke akaụntụ ọ bụla ga-akwụsị inyeghachi onye otu dara ọdịda n'oge na-adịbeghị anya (nnwale TCP a jụrụ, ma ọ bụ 429 sitere na ya) ruo oge na-abawanye okpukpu abụọ mgbe ọdịda ọ bụla mere ọzọ, ruo n'oke enyere. `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-eme ka ọ rụọ ọrụ. |
| `HTTP_PROXY`                             | _(edobeghị)_ | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                              | Proxy HTTP maka oku ndị na-aga n'elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `HTTPS_PROXY`                            | _(edobeghị)_ | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                              | Proxy HTTPS maka oku ndị na-aga n'elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ALL_PROXY`                              | _(edobeghị)_ | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                              | Proxy zuru ụwa ọnụ (na-akwado `socks5://`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_ECHO_URL`               | _(edobeghị)_ | `src/lib/proxyEchoTarget.ts`                 | Na-akpọgide ebe echo-IP nke nnwale ụzọ ọpụpụ proxy na-eji n'otu URL. Mgbe edobeghị ya, nnwale ahụ na-anwale `api64.ipify.org`, mesịa `api4.ipify.org`, ka a ghara ịkọ na njikọ ndị na-akwado naanị IPv4 anwụọla (#9694).                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NO_PROXY`                               | _(edobeghị)_ | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                              | Aha host/IP ndị e ji rikoma kewaa nke ga-agafeghị na proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_DISPATCHER_CONNECTIONS` | `32`         | `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`          | Ọnụọgụ socket kachasị elu nwere ike ịrụ ọrụ n'otu oge maka onye nkesa proxy HTTP/SOCKS ọ bụla echekwara. Stream SSE ndị na-adị ogologo oge dịka Codex `/v1/responses` chọrọ ihe karịrị otu njikọ mgbe ọtụtụ arịrịọ na-ekekọrịta otu proxy nke ọkwa akaụntụ. A ga-amachi ụkpụrụ karịrị `256`.                                                                                                                 |
| `SOCKS_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `10000`      | `open-sse/utils/socksConnectorWithFamily.ts` | Oge njedebe handshake (njikọ) SOCKS5 na ms. Bulie ya mgbe ọtụtụ njikọ n'otu oge kụrụ otu host gateway ụlọ obibi (dịka arịrịọ 100 n'otu oge) — ezigbo handshake nwere ike ịgafe 10s mgbe pool jupụtara, n'agbanyeghị na proxy ka a na-enweta, nke ma ọ bụghị ya ga-apụta dịka ozi ụgha `[Proxy Fast-Fail] Proxy unreachable`. A ga-amachi ya na `120000`.                                                     |
| `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN`                        | `false`      | `src/sse/handlers/chatHelpers.ts`            | Mgbe ọ bụ `false` (ndabara), a ga-**ajụ (fail-closed)** arịrịọ nke proxy e kenyere ya dara n'ịchọpụta ebe ọ ga-aga kama ịlaghachi na njikọ ozugbo — nke a na-egbochi mgbapụ ezigbo IP. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji weghachite ndaghachi DIRECT ochie.                                                                                                                                                        |
| `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT`                 | `false`      | `open-sse/executors`                         | Mee ka akara mkpịsịaka TLS dị ka nke ọzọ site na iji wreq-js (na-eṅomi Chrome 124). Ọ na-egbochi mgbochi JA3/JA4.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `TLS_FINGERPRINT_PROVIDERS`              | _(edobeghị)_ | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`               | Ndepụta ndị na-eweta ọrụ e kwere, nke rikoma kewapụrụ, maka ntụgharị TLS ọhụrụ a na-eme site na proxy (`open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`). TLS kpọmkwem na-ejigide omume ochie ya mgbe edobeghị ya; ọ bụ naanị ndị na-eweta ọrụ ndị a ka a na-ebufe site na njikọ akara mkpịsịaka Chrome-124.                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_TURNSTILE_IGNORE_TLS_ERRORS`  | `false`      | `open-sse/services/claudeTurnstileSolver.ts` | Kwe ka gburugburu ihe nchọgharị Claude Turnstile Playwright leghara njehie asambodo HTTPS anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

### Ọnọdụ dị iche iche

| Ọnọdụ                                                              | Nhazi                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **SOCKS5 site n'ọwara SSH**                                        | `ALL_PROXY=socks5://127.0.0.1:7890`, `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY=true`                                                           |
| **Proxy HTTP nke ụlọ ọrụ**                                         | `HTTP_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `HTTPS_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `NO_PROXY=localhost,internal.corp.com` |
| **Mgbochi akara mkpịsịaka**                                        | `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true` — chọrọ `wreq-js` (agụnyere ya)                                                             |
| **A na-achịkwa ụzọ ọpụpụ / enweghị nnweta kpọmkwem**               | Hapụ `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=false` (ndabara). Arịrịọ ga-ada kpamkpam mgbe proxy adịghị kama ịpụpụ site na njikọ kpọmkwem.       |
| **Usoro ochie / mmepe — kwe ka e jiri njikọ kpọmkwem dochie anya** | `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=true`. Na-eweghachite omume tupu e mesie nchekwa ike: a na-eji njikọ kpọmkwem mgbe mkpebi proxy dara.    |

> **Rịba ama (ịgafe nkwado NVIDIA — #3226):** Ebe njedebe NVIDIA maka nkwado API-key
> na-akwụsị mgbe e zigara ya site na fetch zuru ụwa ọnụ nke proxy/TLS gbanwere (undici dispatcher → 504).
> `src/lib/providers/validation.ts::directHttpsRequest()` kpachaara anya na-agafe
> mgbanwe proxy maka naanị otu oku nkwado ahụ site n'iji `safeOutboundFetch({ bypassProxyPatch: true })`.
> Nke a bụ mwepu edekọtara nke nwere oke akọwapụtara — ọ **naghị** emetụta ụzọ ọpụpụ nkata/ojiji.
> `tests/unit/proxy-bypass-scope-guard-3226.test.ts` na-eme ka oke ngafe a ghara ịgbanwe.

---

## 9. Njikọ Ngwaọrụ CLI

Na-achịkwa etu OmniRoute si achọpụta ma malite sidecar CLI (Claude Code, Codex, wdg.).

| Mgbanwe                           | Ndabara                          | Faịlụ Isi Mbido                                     | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| --------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_MODE`                        | `auto`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | `auto` = chọọ na PATH sistemụ; `manual` = jiri naanị ụzọ ndị akọwapụtara kpọmkwem.                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CLI_EXTRA_PATHS`                 | _(edobeghị)_                     | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ndenye PATH ndị ọzọ maka ịchọta faịlụ binary CLI (e ji kọlọn kewaa ha).                                                                                                                                                                                           |
