| `CLI_CONFIG_HOME`                 | _(edobeghị)_                     | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Dochie ndekọ ụlọ eji agụ nhazi CLI (`~/.claude`, `~/.codex`). Ọ ga-abụrịrị ụzọ zuru ezu ma dịkwa n'ime ụlọ usoro ahụ — **ma ọ bụ**, n'ime container, ụzọ e ji bind-mount tinye (ọ bụ otu a ka `/host-home` si arụ ọrụ). Ihe ọ bụla ọzọ ga-alaghachi na ndekọ ụlọ. |
| `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`         | `true`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Kwe ka OmniRoute dee faịlụ nhazi CLI (mmelite token, data session). Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji mee ka ide ọ bụla na nhazi CLI daa, tinyere njehie doro anya nke sịrị "agbanyụrụ ide ihe".                                                                       |
| `CLI_CLAUDE_BIN`                  | `claude`                         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary Claude CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CLI_CODEX_BIN`                   | `codex`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary Codex CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_DROID_BIN`                   | `droid`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary Droid CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_OPENCLAW_BIN`                | `openclaw`                       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary OpenClaw CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CLI_CURSOR_BIN`                  | `agent`, emesịa `cursor`         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary agent nke Cursor. Ọ bụrụ na ọ dịghị, nchọpụta ga-ebu ụzọ nwaa `agent`, ma ọ daa, jiri `cursor`.                                                                                                                              |
| `CLI_CLINE_BIN`                   | `cline`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary Cline CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_5DIVE_BIN`                   | `5dive`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary 5dive CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_5DIVE_STATE_DIR`             | `/var/lib/5dive`                 | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ndekọ ọnọdụ sistemụ nke 5dive (profaịlụ nkwenye nke root nwe); ọ na-eme ka ndabara STATE_DIR nke 5dive kwekọọ.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLI_CONTINUE_BIN`                | `cn`                             | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary Continue CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CLI_QODER_BIN`                   | `qodercli`                       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary Qoder CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_QWEN_BIN`                    | `qwen`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary Qwen Code CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CLI_AIDER_BIN`                   | `aider`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary Aider CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_GOOSE_BIN`                   | `goose`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary Goose CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_GEMINI_BIN`                  | `gemini`                         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary Google Gemini CLI — naanị maka nchọpụta/nlele ahụike n'akụkụ server; `omniroute run gemini` na-achọta faịlụ binary `gemini` site na PATH sistemụ.                                                                            |
| `CLI_KILO_BIN`                    | `kilocode`                       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary Kilo Code CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CLI_OPENCODE_BIN`                | `opencode`                       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary OpenCode CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CLI_HERMES_BIN`                  | `hermes`                         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary Hermes. Ihe ndenye katalọgụ abụọ (`hermes` na `hermes-agent`) na-ejikwa ya.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CLI_FORGE_BIN`                   | `forge`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary ForgeCode CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

| `ZCODE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`          | `250`                            | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Nkeji oge nyocha ugboro ugboro (ms) maka mmecha ntụgharị ZCode.                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `HERMES_HOME`                     | `~/.hermes`                      | `src/lib/cli-helper/config-generator/hermesHome.ts` | Ndekọ ebe obibi Hermes Agent ebe OmniRoute na-agụ/na-ede nhazi Hermes CLI. Ọ dabara na env var nke ihe nrụnye Hermes PowerShell na-edobe na Windows (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\hermes`).                                                                                    |

### Mmekọrịta Akpaghị Aka nke Profaịlụ CLI

A naghị agbanye ọkọlọtọ atụmatụ ndị a na ndabara; a ga-eji aka họrọ ịgbanye ha. A pụkwara ịgbanye ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ ha site na
dashboard CLI Code.

| Mgbanwe                               | Ndabara | Faịlụ Isi                                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CODEX_PROFILES`  | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Mgbe mmekọrịta ụdị onye na-enye ọrụ gasịrị, ọ na-eji katalọgụ dị ugbu a degharịa faịlụ profaịlụ `~/.codex/*.config.toml` na-akpaghị aka. Ọ chọrọ `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-agbanwe nhazi Codex nọ n'ọrụ/nke ndabara, nkwenye njirimara, ntọala Codex-lb, ma ọ bụ nhọrọ onye na-enye ọrụ.              |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CLAUDE_PROFILES` | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Mgbe mmekọrịta ụdị onye na-enye ọrụ gasịrị, ọ na-eji katalọgụ dị ugbu a degharịa faịlụ profaịlụ Claude Code `~/.claude/profiles/<name>/settings.json` na-akpaghị aka. Ọ chọrọ `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-agbanwe nhazi Claude nọ n'ọrụ/nke ndabara, nkwenye njirimara, ma ọ bụ nhọrọ onye na-enye ọrụ. |

### Ọmụmaatụ Docker

```bash
# Tinye binary ndị dị na kọmputa nnabata n'ime konteena ma gwa OmniRoute ebe ha nọ:
CLI_EXTRA_PATHS=/host-cli/bin
CLI_CONFIG_HOME=/host-home
CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES=true
CLI_CLAUDE_BIN=/host-cli/bin/claude
```

`CLI_CONFIG_HOME` na-amalite ịrụ ọrụ naanị mgbe e mere ka ụzọ ahụ bụrụ bind mount sitere na
host — jikọta ya na mount dịka `~/.codex:/host-home/.codex:rw` (lee profaịlụ
`host` dị na `docker-compose.yml`). A na-eleghara ụzọ na-adịghị n'ime home
onye ọrụ container ma ọ bụ na-abụghị bind mount anya, n'ihi na a ga-atụfu ihe
e dere ebe ahụ mgbe e megharịrị container ahụ.

Image ahụ na-arụ ọrụ dịka `USER node`, ya mere `/root` na-enweghị mount abụghị
override ziri ezi.

| Mgbanwe                                  | Ndabara         | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                   | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                    |
| ---------------------------------------- | --------------- | ----------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTAINER`                    | _(akpaghị aka)_ | `src/shared/utils/containerEnv.ts`  | Manye nchọpụta container ka ọ dị ọkụ (`1`/`true`) ma ọ bụ ka ọ gbanyụọ (`0`/`false`). A chọrọ nke a naanị na runtime ndị nchọpụta akpaghị aka na-ahụghị. |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CONTAINER_CONFIG_WRITE` | `false`         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` | Kwe ka CLI-tool dee nhazi n'ụzọ container na-enweghị mount n'agbanyeghị nke ahụ. Ihe dakọtara na CLI bụ `--allow-container-write`.                       |

### Ngwa enyemaka Binary CLI (`omniroute`)

Mgbanwe ndị a na-ahazi omume nke binary CLI `omniroute` n'onwe ya (ọ bụghị
nchọpụta sidecar dị n'elu).

| Mgbanwe                      | Ndabara        | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta                       | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ---------------------------- | -------------- | --------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_LANG`             | _(sistemu)_    | `bin/cli/i18n.mjs`                      | Manye asụsụ mmepụta CLI. Locale BCP-47 (dịka `en`, `pt-BR`). Ọ na-akagbu env vars locale sistemu (LC_ALL, LC_MESSAGES).                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_SHOW_LOG`         | _(edobebeghị)_ | `bin/cli/runtime/processSupervisor.mjs` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji zipụ stdout/stderr nke server gaa na terminal n'ụdị nlekọta. Ọ dakọtara na flag `--log` na `omniroute serve`.                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_TOKEN`        | _(edobebeghị)_ | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Token machine-auth etinyere dịka header `x-omniroute-cli-token`. A na-emepụta ya na-akpaghị aka na task 8.12.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_MS`  | `30000`        | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Ogologo oge HTTP maka nnwale ọ bụla (ms) maka arịrịọ CLI → server.                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_READY_TIMEOUT_MS` | `60000`        | `bin/cli/utils/pid.mjs`                 | Oge kachasị (ms) CLI ga-echere endpoint ahụike server tupu o bipụta ịdọ aka ná ntị timeout. Ọ bara uru maka cold start ndị na-egbu oge (dịka Windows). A pụkwara ịtọ ya site na `--ready-timeout`.                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VERBOSE`          | `0`            | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji bipụta nchọpụta retry/backoff na stderr mgbe iwu CLI na-arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH`      | _(edobebeghị)_ | `bin/cli/plugins.mjs`                   | Directory ahaziri iche maka nchọpụta plugin CLI (ngwugwu `omniroute-cmd-*`). Mgbe edobebeghị ya, ndabara ya bụ `~/.omniroute/plugins/`. Ọ bụ naanị maka CLI — ọ naghị erute plugin scanner nke server, nke `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR` na-atụ aka na ya (ngalaba 2). |

## 10. Njikọ Agent Dị N'ime & MCP

| Ngbanwe                                         | Ndabara                                             | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                                               | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_URL`                            | nchọpụta-akpaaka                                    | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | URL akọwapụtara nke ngwaọrụ MCP/A2A ga-eji ruo OmniRoute. Ọ na-akagbu nchọpụta-akpaaka localhost.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`                             | _(edoghị)_                                          | Modul MCP/A2A                                               | Igodo API maka oku ngwaọrụ MCP dị n'ime na oku nka A2A.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_ID`                          | _(edoghị)_                                          | `open-sse/mcp-server/audit.ts`                              | ID igodo maka ikenye ndekọ nyocha MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ROUTER_API_KEY`                                | _(edoghị)_                                          | Ihe ochie                                                   | Aha ọzọ ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_HISTORY_RETENTION_DAYS`          | `30`                                                | `src/lib/a2a/taskManager.ts`                                | Ụbọchị ole a ga-edobe akụkọ ihe mere eme nke ọrụ A2A na nchekwa data mpaghara tupu nhichapụ kwa ụbọchị ehichapụ ahịrị. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, ọ bụghị ọnụọgụ, ma ọ bụ `<= 0`, ọ ga-alaghachi na `30`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_MEMORY_HITS`                     | `1`                                                 | `src/lib/a2a/taskExecution.ts`                              | Mgbanwe nkwụsị mberede maka njirimara nleba anya memory-hits nke A2A. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji mafere nyocha ncheta ebe nchekwa maka ọrụ kpamkpam; uru ọ bụla ọzọ (gụnyere mgbe edoghị ya) na-eme ka ọ nọgide na-arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_ENABLED`                 | `false`                                             | `src/app/api/issue-agent/runs/route.ts`                     | Na-eme ka endpoint nke Issue Agent na-anọghị n'ịntanetị/mpaghara maka recorded-triage rụọ ọrụ. Hapụ ya ka ọ ghara ịrụ ọrụ belụsọ mgbe a na-eme usoro ọrụ recorded-triage mpaghara kpọmkwem.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_TIMEOUT_MS`              | _(edoghị)_                                          | `src/lib/issueAgent/execution.ts`                           | Oge ngwụcha (ms) maka otu ọsọ recorded-triage nke Issue Agent. A na-amachi ya n'ogo kachasị elu dị n'ime; ọ na-alaghachi na ndabara arụnyere mgbe edoghị ya ma ọ bụ mgbe ọ na-ezighi ezi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT`                             | _(context na-arụ ọrụ)_                              | `bin/cli/program.mjs`, `bin/cli/api.mjs`                    | Context/profaịlụ ọnọdụ-ime-anya nke CLI maka iwu `omniroute`; ọ na-akagbu context na-arụ ọrụ n'ebe nchekwa context mpaghara. Ọ bụ otu ihe na `--context <name>`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT_KEYCHAIN_DISABLED`           | `0`                                                 | `bin/cli/contexts.mjs`                                      | Gbanyụọ backend keychain OS `keytar` nhọrọ maka nzere context CLI. Mgbe agbanyere ya, nzere na-anọgide na ọnọdụ `0600` nke `config.json`, CLI na-ewepụkwa ịdọ aka ná ntị fallback otu ugboro; ezubere ya maka ọrụ headless/container a kpachapụrụ anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_ENFORCE_SCOPES`                  | `true`                                              | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Manye njikwa ohere dabere na scope na oku ngwaọrụ MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_SCOPES`                          | _(niile)_                                           | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Scope ndị e ji rikoma kewaa: `admin`, `combos`, `health`, `models`, `routing`, `budget`, `metrics`, `pricing`, `memory`, `skills`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_COMPRESS_DESCRIPTIONS`           | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Mpịakọta nkọwa ngwaọrụ MCP tupu a hazie manifest ahụ n'usoro serialization. Uru ndị na-agbanye ya: `1`, `true`, `on`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_DESCRIPTION_COMPRESSION`         | `rtk`                                               | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Algọridim/profaịlụ mpịakọta. Uru ndị na-agbanyụ ya: `0`, `false`, `off`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                | `10000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Oke oge nkwụsị (ms) maka ọgụgụ njikwa dị n'ime MCP-server (ahụike, nkwụsi ike, combos, oke ojiji, ojiji).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_UPSTREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `60000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Oke oge tupu a kwụsị (ms) maka ngafe MCP ndị na-echere onye na-eweta (`route_request`, `web_search`, `web_fetch`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE`                   | `5`                                                 | `src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`                         | Ọnụọgụ kachasị nke ihe atụ ndeksi local-corpus echekwara na ebe nchekwa (LRU, otu maka ndekọ mgbọrọgwụ ọ bụla e tinyere na ndeksi). A na-amanye ya ka ọ ghara ịdị n'okpuru `1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `MODEL_SYNC_INTERVAL_HOURS`                     | `24`                                                | `src/shared/services/modelSyncScheduler.ts`                 | Etiti oge mmekọrịta katalọgụ modelụ n'ime awa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES`         | `70`                                                | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Etiti oge a na-enyocha oke ọsọ na oke ojiji nke onye na-eweta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_SPACING_MS`               | `1500`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Oghere oge (ms) n'etiti nnweta oke OAuth ndị na-esochi ibe ha n'ime mmekọrịta buru ibu; a na-enweta njikọ OAuth otu otu iji zere iziga ọtụtụ arịrịọ n'otu oge na upstream. `0` na-ewepụ ya (n'otu oge).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS`         | `250`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Etiti oge kacha nta (ms) n'etiti nnweta oke upstream ndị na-esochi ibe ha n'ụzọ nyocha-mbụ/nlekota maka arịrịọ ọ bụla; ọ na-ekesa oku netwọkụ ndị na-eme n'otu oge ka ọtụtụ akaụntụ nọ n'otu IP ghara iziga ọtụtụ arịrịọ n'otu oge na upstream. Ejikọtara ya na ndị na-enweta oke Codex (`/wham/usage`), DeepSeek, Bailian (ebe nnweta abụọ ahụ), OpenCode, na Crof (#6009, #6911). Ụzọ izipu izugbe `usage.ts::getUsageForProvider` (github/glm/minimax/nanogpt/xai/wdg.) akabeghị ekpuchi — a na-esochi ya iche. Ọ naghị emetụta nnweta cache. `0` na-agbanyụ ya; a na-amanye ya ịnọ n'agbata `0..5000`. |

| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_THRESHOLD`        | `3`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Ọnụọgụ ọdịda na-aga n'ihu n'arịrịọ dị iche iche tupu breaker nke engine emeghee.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_COOLDOWN_MS`      | `30000`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Milisekọnd ole a ga na-amafere engine mepere emepe tupu nnwale half-open.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `COMPRESSION_CCR_RETRIEVAL_RAMP_FACTOR`         | `2`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/ccr/index.ts`        | Ramp nzaghachi-nweghachite T08/H8 CCR: nweghachite ọ bụla gara aga nke ngọngọ echekwara na-ebuli `minChars` dị irè ya n'usoro linear (a na-akpakọ ọdịnaya a na-eweghachite ugboro ugboro obere; nweghachite `>=3` = agaghị akpakọ ya ma ọlị). `1` na-agbanyụọ ramp ahụ (naanị ịmafe binary n'ókè ahụ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `COMPRESSION_CCR_DURABLE_STORE`                 | `true`                                              | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/ccr/index.ts`        | Ebe nchekwa blọk CCR na-adịgide adịgide (#9061). Ọ na-eji SQLite kwado ebe nchekwa dị na ebe nchekwa kọmputa ka blọk wee lanarị nchụpụ LRU, TTL, mmalitegharị, ma ọ bụ iweghachite nke rutere na instance ọzọ. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji debe blọk naanị n'ebe nchekwa kọmputa. Blọk karịrị 512KB na runtime igwe ojii ga-anọgide na ebe nchekwa kọmputa naanị n'agbanyeghị nke ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

| `COMPRESSION_PREFIX_FREEZE_THRESHOLD`           | `3`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/prefixFreeze.ts`             | Ọnụọgụ ugboro a ga-ahụ system prompt tupu ewere ya dị ka prefix kwụsiri ike efriziri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

Ọ na-achịkwa ma setup wizard ga-apụta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_BODY_PROJECT_OVERRIDE`         | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/executors/antigravity.ts`                         | Ụzọ mgbapụ: kwe ka body arịrịọ dochie field project Antigravity.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_CREDITS`                           | `off`                                               | `open-sse/services/antigravityCredits.ts`                   | Iwu kredit Google One AI: `off` anaghị etinye kredit mgbe ọ bụla, `retry` na-etinye ya otu ugboro mgbe quota 429 tozuru etozu gasịrị, ebe `always` na-etinye ya n'arịrịọ mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

Uru na-abụghị efu na-eme ka ngafe ahụ rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `AGY_TOKEN_FILE`                                | `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/antigravity-oauth-token` | `src/app/api/providers/agy-auth/apply-local/route.ts`       | Dochie path faịlụ token Antigravity CLI (agy) maka mbubata nbanye local nke nchọpụta-akpaghị aka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

### Njikọ OAuth CLI (Ime)

| Mgbanwe             | Ndabara              | Faịlụ Isi                       | Nkọwa                                        |
| ------------------- | -------------------- | ------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`  | nchọpụta-akpaghị aka | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | URL server maka njikọ nkwenye CLI↔OmniRoute. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`   | _(edobeghị)_         | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Token nkwenye maka njikọ CLI.                |
| `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID` | `cli`                | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | ID onye ọrụ maka session njikọ CLI.          |
| `SERVER_URL`        | _(edobeghị)_         | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha nnọchi ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`.    |
| `CLI_TOKEN`         | _(edobeghị)_         | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha nnọchi ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`.     |
| `CLI_USER_ID`       | _(edobeghị)_         | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha nnọchi ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID`.   |

## 11. Nzere Onye Na-enye OAuth

Nzere arụnyere n'ime maka **mmepe localhost**. Maka mbunye n'ebe dịpụrụ adịpụ, debanye aha nzere nke gị na console ndị nrụpụta nke onye na-enye ọ bụla.

| Mgbanwe                           | Onye na-enye            | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| --------------------------------- | ----------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLAUDE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`          | Claude Code (Anthropic) | Klayentị ọha — ọ dịghị nzuzo achọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REDIRECT_URI`        | Claude Code             | Dochie URI ntụgharị. Ndabara: `https://platform.claude.com/oauth/code/callback`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CODEX_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`           | Codex / OpenAI          | Klayentị ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`          | Gemini (Google)         | Ọ chọrọ `_SECRET` kwekọrọ na ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`      | Gemini (Google)         | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `KIMI_CODING_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`     | Kimi Coding (Moonshot)  | Klayentị ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`     | Antigravity (Google)    | Ọ chọrọ `_SECRET` kwekọrọ na ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET` | Antigravity (Google)    | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`          | GitHub Copilot          | Klayentị ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `GHE_COPILOT_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`     | GHE Copilot             | Nhọrọ iji dochie ID klayentị OAuth nke GitHub Enterprise Copilot. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, ọ na-eji ndabara ọha nke `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `WINDSURF_API_KEY`                | Windsurf / Devin (v3.8) | API key ndabere nke `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` na-eji mgbe enweghị nzere maka njikọ ahụ. Ọ bụ nhọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                   | Devin CLI (v3.8)        | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary Devin CLI (`devin`). `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` na-achọpụta ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`      | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | ID klayentị OAuth maka GitLab Duo. Debanye aha ngwa na `https://gitlab.com/-/profile/applications` site na iji URI ntụgharị `<NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL>/callback` na oke ikike `api, read_user, openid, profile, email`. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, ọ na-eji `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`  | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Nzuzo klayentị OAuth maka GitLab Duo. Ọ bụ nhọrọ — usoro PKCE anaghị achọ nzuzo. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, ọ na-eji `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`             | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Dochie URL ntọala GitLab (GitLab a na-akwado n'onwe). Ndabara ya bụ `https://gitlab.com`. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, ọ na-eji `GITLAB_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `GITLAB_BASE_URL`                 | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Ndabere ochie maka `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`. A na-eji ya mgbe edoghị ụdị `_DUO_`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`          | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Ndabere ochie maka `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` nke `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` na-eji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`      | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Nhọrọ ndabere ochie maka `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET` nke `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` na-eji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`       | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `QODER_OAUTH_AUTHORIZE_URL`       | Qoder                   | Tọọ ya iji mee ka Qoder OAuth rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `QODER_OAUTH_TOKEN_URL`           | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `QODER_OAUTH_USERINFO_URL`        | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`           | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `QODER_PERSONAL_ACCESS_TOKEN`     | Qoder                   | Nhọrọ ndabere igodo API ozugbo (na-agafeghị OAuth).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`             | Qoder                   | ID ebe ọrụ maka Qoder CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_QODER_WORKSPACE`       | Qoder                   | Aha ọzọ maka `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `QODER_CLI_CONFIG_DIR`            | Qoder                   | Dochie ndekọ nhazi Qoder CLI (nnọkọ PAT nọọrọ onwe ya, na-egbochi idegharị nbanye ihe nchọgharị).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `BLACKBOX_WEB_VALIDATED_TOKEN`    | Blackbox Web            | Token `tk` nke ihu-ngwa a ga-eziga dịka `validated` na `/api/chat`. A chọrọ ya mgbe Blackbox na-amanye ndakọrịta token; ma ọ bụghị ya, OmniRoute ga-eji UUID e mepụtara na-enweghị usoro dịka nhọrọ ndabere. Lee nsogbu #2252.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_BASE_URL`          | Nchedo Vision Bridge    | URL ntọala dakọtara na OpenAI maka oku vision-bridge ndị na-abụghị nke Anthropic. Na ndabara, ọ na-eji env URL OpenAI ochie ma ọ bụ api.openai.com. Tụnye ya na self-loop `/v1` nke OmniRoute ma ọ bụ endpoint ọ bụla dakọtara na OpenAI (Gemini OpenAI-compat, OpenRouter). Nsogbu #2232. Mgbe URL ahụ bụ `/v1` nke OmniRoute n'onwe ya, arịrịọ nta describe na-eziga `x-omniroute-admission-bypass: internal` ma jiri nzere self-loop a chọpụtara (`sk_omniroute` sentinel na ọnọdụ local, ma ọ bụ `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY` / `ROUTER_API_KEY` — #1350) mee nkwenye njirimara ka nrụnye `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` wee rụọ ọrụ. |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_API_KEY`           | Nchedo Vision Bridge    | Igodo API maka URL dị n'elu. Ọ na-edochi env vars OpenAI / Google nke onye na-eweta ọ bụla maka oku vision-bridge ndị na-abụghị nke Anthropic. Model Anthropic na-edobe ụzọ igodo Anthropic pụrụ iche ha. Nsogbu #2232.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

> [!WARNING]
>
> 1. Gaa na [Google Cloud Console → Credentials](https://console.cloud.google.com/apis/credentials)
> 2. Mepụta OAuth 2.0 Client ID (ụdị: "Ngwa Weebụ")
> 3. Tinye URL sava gị dịka URI ntụgharị e nyere ikike
> 4. Dochie uru nzere ndị dị na `.env`.

## 12. Mgbanwe User-Agent nke Onye Na-eweta Ọrụ

Gbanwee isiokwu `User-Agent` a na-eziga nye onye na-eweta ọrụ upstream ọ bụla. Klas ntọala executor na-achọpụta nke a n'oge mmemme na-agba ọsọ:

```
process.env[`${PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`]
```

> **Isi mmalite:** `open-sse/executors/base.ts` → `buildHeaders()`

| Mgbanwe | Uru Ndabara | Mgbe a ga-emelite ya |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` | `claude-cli/2.1.258 (external, cli)` | Mgbe Anthropic wepụtara ụdị CLI ọhụrụ |
| `CLAUDE_DISABLE_TOOL_NAME_CLOAK` | `false` | `executors/base.ts` + `executors/cliproxyapi.ts` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1`/`true` iji zipụ aha ngwaọrụ harness nke ndị ọzọ dịka ha dị na Anthropic n'ụzọ abụọ ahụ na-eduga na Anthropic (OAuth nke mbụ na CLIProxyAPI). Na ndabara, executor na-enye aha ọzọ n'ụzọ a pụrụ ịkọwa mgbe niile nye aha ngwaọrụ ndị na-abụghị nke Claude Code (na-eji mapping canonical nke Claude Code ebe ọ dị, ma ọ bụghị ya, PascalCase), ma tụgharịa ha azụ na nzaghachi site na `_toolNameMap`, ka a ghara ịjụ harnesses nwere ngwaọrụ snake_case dịka clients ndị ọzọ a matara site na fingerprint. Maka debugging naanị. |
| `CODEX_USER_AGENT` | `codex-cli/0.142.0 (Windows 10.0.26200; x64)` | Mgbe OpenAI melitere Codex CLI |
| `CODEX_CLIENT_VERSION` | `0.131.0` | Gbanwee ụdị Codex client n'adabereghị na eriri UA zuru ezu |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` | `2.1.258` | Gbanwee ụdị Claude Code a na-akpọsa n'adabereghị na `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT`. Anthropic na-egbochi ụfọdụ models dabere na uru a (#12417). |
| `GITHUB_COPILOT_CLI_VERSION` | `1.0.81-6` | Gbanwee ụdị Copilot CLI a na-akpọsa n'adabereghị na `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` |
| `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` | `GitHubCopilotChat/0.54.0` | Mgbe GitHub Copilot Chat melitere |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_USER_AGENT` | `antigravity/2.0.1 darwin/arm64` | Mgbe Antigravity IDE melitere |
| `KIRO_USER_AGENT` | `AWS-SDK-JS/3.0.0 kiro-ide/1.0.0` | Mgbe Kiro IDE melitere |
| `KIRO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` | `kiro-cli` | Gbanwee `clientId` (id ọha) nke social device-code Kiro |
| `KIRO_VERIFY_FULL_CRC` | `false` | Nhọrọ ịbanye: nkwado CRC zuru ezu maka frame ọ bụla na Kiro event stream (iji debug streams mebiri emebi) |
| `QODER_USER_AGENT` | `Qoder-Cli` | Mgbe Qoder CLI melitere |
| `CURSOR_USER_AGENT` | `Cursor/3.3` | Mgbe Cursor melitere |

> [!TIP]
> Ị nwere ike ịgbakwunye mgbanwe User-Agent maka onye na-eweta ọrụ **ọ bụla** site n'iji ụkpụrụ `{PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`. Executor na-emepụta aha env var ahụ n'ụzọ na-agbanwe agbanwe.
