## 13. Ndakọrịta Akara-Mkpịsịaka CLI

Mgbe agbanyere ya, OmniRoute na-ahazigharị isiokwu HTTP na mpaghara ahụ JSON ka ha kwekọọ kpọmkwem na akara njirimara nke ngwa CLI gọọmentị. Nke a na-ebelata ihe ize ndụ nke ịka akaụntụ gị akara ma na-echekwa IP proxy gị.

**Isi mmalite:** `open-sse/config/cliFingerprints.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`

### Maka Onye Na-enye Ọrụ Ọ Bụla

| Mgbanwe                  | Mgbalite | Mmetụta                              |
| ------------------------ | -------- | ------------------------------------ |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CODEX`       | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Codex CLI      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLAUDE`      | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Claude Code    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_GITHUB`      | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ GitHub Copilot |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ANTIGRAVITY` | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Antigravity    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CURSOR`      | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Cursor         |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIMI_CODING` | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Kimi Coding    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KILOCODE`    | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Kilo Code      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLINE`       | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Cline          |

### Nke Izugbe

| Mgbanwe          | Mgbalite | Mmetụta                                                                 |
| ---------------- | -------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ALL` | `=1`     | Gbaa ndakọrịta akara-mkpịsịaka maka ndị na-enye ọrụ **niile** otu mgbe. |

### Mgbanwe njirimara Kimi Coding CLI

| Mgbanwe                 | Nke ndabara          | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta                        | Nkọwa                                                            |
| ----------------------- | -------------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KIMI_CLI_VERSION`      | `1.36.0`             | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Dochie ụdị Kimi CLI a na-eziga n'oge oku OAuth/API.              |
| `KIMI_CODING_DEVICE_ID` | _(ndabara e jidere)_ | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Dochie ID ngwaọrụ Kimi e jidere nke a na-eji n'isiokwu klayenti. |

> [!NOTE]
> Njirimara a na-arụkọ ọrụ na mgbanwe User-Agent (§12). Sistemụ akara-mkpịsịaka na-ahụ maka nhazi isiokwu na nhazi mpaghara ahụ, ebe mgbanwe User-Agent na-ahụ maka eriri UA akọwapụtara. Enwere ike ịgbanye ha abụọ n'adabereghị onwe ha.

---

## 14. Ndị Na-enye Ọrụ Igodo API

Igodo API maka ndị na-enye ọrụ na-eji nyocha njirimara ozugbo. **Nhazi akwadoro:** Dashboard → Providers → Add API Key.

Ịtọ ha site na mgbanwe gburugburu bụ ụzọ ọzọ maka mbugharị Docker ma ọ bụ mbugharị na-enweghị ihu njikwa.

Ụkpụrụ a na-amata: `{PROVIDER_ID}_API_KEY`

| Mgbanwe            | Onye na-enye ọrụ                                     |
| ------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------- |
| `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` | DeepSeek                                             |
| `NVIDIA_API_KEY`   | NVIDIA NIM                                           |
| `JINA_AI_API_KEY`  | Jina AI (Foundation API + Reader ndabere)            |
| `JINA_API_KEY`     | Jina AI (aha ọzọ maka `JINA_AI_API_KEY`)             |
| `GEMINI_API_KEY`   | Gemini (Google AI Studio) embeddings + ndabere nkata |
| `GOOGLE_API_KEY`   | Gemini (aha ọzọ maka `GEMINI_API_KEY`)               |

> [!NOTE]
> E wepụrụ ndenye `${PROVIDER}_API_KEY` kwụ ọtọ maka Groq, xAI, Mistral, Perplexity, Together AI, Fireworks, Cerebras, Cohere, Nebius, na Qianfan na v3.8.0 n'ihi na runtime anaghịzi agụ ha — ndị na-enye ọrụ ahụ na-adabere naanị na Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / DB ezoro ezo. Hụ ngalaba _Nnyocha: Mgbanwe E Wepụrụ / Ndị Na-adịghị Arụ Ọrụ_ dị na njedebe akwụkwọ a maka ụzọ mbugharị.

> [!TIP]
> A na-echekwa igodo ndị edobere site na Dashboard n'ụdị ezoro ezo na SQLite, ha na-ebutekwa mgbanwe gburugburu ụzọ.
>
> **Jina:** embeddings `jina-ai/…`, rerank, classify, segment, na `jina-search` anaghị akwụ ụgwọ igodo env klọsta mgbe njikọ dashboard `jina-ai` (ma ọ bụ `jina-reader` a na-ekekọrịta) dị — `getProviderCredentials` na-ebu ụzọ mejupụta. A na-eji `JINA_AI_API_KEY` / `JINA_API_KEY` naanị mgbe enweghị igodo dashboard a pụrụ iji. Ndekọ oku na-egosi ndabere env dịka `connection_id=env:JINA_AI_API_KEY`. Kaadị Reader (`jina-reader`, `r.jina.ai`) anaghị enye `/v1/embeddings` ma ọ bụ `/v1/rerank`.
>
> **Gemini:** `gemini/gemini-embedding-2` (aha ọzọ `google/gemini-embedding-2`) na-ebu ụzọ jiri njikọ dashboard `gemini`. A na-eji `GEMINI_API_KEY` / `GOOGLE_API_KEY` naanị mgbe enweghị igodo dashboard a pụrụ iji. Ndekọ oku na-egosi ndabere env dịka `connection_id=env:GEMINI_API_KEY`. Okporo data multimodal nke izizi na-eji `x-goog-api-key` megide `:embedContent` / `:batchEmbedContents` — ihe N OpenAI `input` na-aghọ vector N.

---

## 15. Ntọala Oge Njedebe

Ụkpụrụ niile dị na **milisekọnd**. A na-eme mkpebi etiti na `src/shared/utils/runtimeTimeouts.ts`.

### Usoro Ndị Isi Oge Njedebe

```
REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (mkpuchi zuru ụwa ọnụ)
├─→ FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS (oku ndị na-eweta ozi dị n'elu, ndabara: 600000)
│   ├─→ FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   │   └── TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS (nwere onwe ya, ndabara: 10000)
│   ├── RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS (nwere onwe ya, ndabara: 15000)
│   ├── FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS (nwere onwe ya, ndabara: 30000)
│   └── FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (nwere onwe ya, ndabara: 4000)
├─→ STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, ndabara: 600000)
├─→ STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (nwere onwe ya, ndabara: 1260000; 0 na-agbanyụ ya)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, ndabara: 80000)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS (na-amachi mgbatị njikere na-agbanwe agbanwe, ndabara: 180000)
└─→ API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, ndabara: 30000)
    ├─→ API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (e sitere na ya nweta, ndabara: 300000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (ndabara: 60000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (ndabara: 5000)
    └── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS (ndabara: 0 = agbanyụrụ)
```

| Mgbanwe                                         | Ndabara                                                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`                            | _(edoghị)_                                                        | Ụzọ mkpirisi zuru ụwa ọnụ — na-akagbu ndabara nke ma `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` ma `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                              | `600000`                                                          | Ngụkọta oge nchere arịrịọ HTTP maka oku ndị na-eweta ọrụ upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                        | `600000`                                                          | Ogologo oge ịgbachi nkịtị kacha elu n'etiti byte upstream raw tupu a kwụsị ya. Model ndị na-eche echiche ogologo oge anaghị akwụsịkarị >90s.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `1260000`                                                         | Ogologo ndụ kacha elu nke stream SSE na-arụ ọrụ; ọ naghị amalitegharị mgbe byte upstream bịara, ọ dabereghịkwa na `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`. E si na `timeoutMs` kachasị ukwuu nke model ọ bụla dị na registry (1200000, Codex) tinyere oke mgbakwunye 60000 nweta ya, ka a ghara ịkwụsị model e nyere ohere iji mmefu oge ya niile n'etiti azịza. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_SSE_COMMENTS`                        | _(agbanyụrụ)_                                                     | Ma OmniRoute nwere ike izipu ahịrị nkọwa SSE `:` (dịka heartbeat `: keepalive` na metadata trailer `x-omniroute-*`). A na-agbanyụ ya na ndabara (#10524) n’ihi na client ndị na-agbasosi ndakọrịta OpenAI ike na-eji JSON.parse hazie ahịrị SSE ọ bụla ma daa mgbe ha hụrụ nkọwa `:`; nke a anaghị emetụta heartbeat `data:`. Tọọ `on`/`true`/`1`/`yes` iji họrọ ịlaghachi na ya. `open-sse/utils/sseHeartbeat.ts` na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                    |
| `STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `80000`                                                           | Oge iji nata event SSE mbụ na-abụghị ping. Ọ na-eketa `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` mgbe edobere ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `180000`                                                          | Oke windo nchere mgbanwe maka event mbụ n’arịrịọ streaming buru ibu, juputara na tool, ma ọ bụ chọrọ reasoning dị elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_POLICY_ENABLED`           | `true`                                                            | Kill-switch maka heuristic `/goal`. Tọọ `false`/`0`/`off` iji gbanyụọ nchọpụta kpamkpam — a naghị ebuli oge nchere readiness na mgbake stream site na body/header arịrịọ, nke na-ebelata mmụba oge nchere client na-achịkwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS` | `600000`                                                          | Oke windo nchere maka event mbụ n’ọsọ agent `/goal` achọpụtara ma ọ bụ arịrịọ ejiri `x-omniroute-agent-goal` manye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_STREAM_RECOVERY`          | `true`                                                            | Gbaa mgbake stream n’oge na-akpaghị aka maka ọsọ agent `/goal` achọpụtara. Tọọ `false`/`0`/`off` iji gbanyụọ nhọrọ pụrụ iche nke goal. Nke a nwere ike naanị ỊTỤKWASỊ mgbake n’elu ndabara onye njikwa — ọ naghị akagbu nhọrọ ịpụ akọwapụtara nke ọma na ntọala `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`/DB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_DROP_NONSTANDARD_EVENTS`       | `true`                                                            | Wepụ event SSE `codex.*` na-abụghị ọkọlọtọ (dịka `codex.rate_limits`) nke na-eme ka `responses.stream()` nke OpenAI SDK daa site na 502. Agbanyere ya na ndabara (#11014). Tọọ `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` iji zipụ ha n’ihu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS`                  | _(edoghị)_                                                        | Transport app-server Codex a na-ahọrọ ịbanye na ya. Endpoint WebSocket (`ws://`/`wss://`) nke sidecar `codex app-server` mpaghara. Mgbe edobere ya na token, a na-ebuga arịrịọ Codex site na JSON-RPC gaa na sidecar kama iji HTTP Responses API. A pụkwara ịtọ ya maka connection ọ bụla site na `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerUrl`. `open-sse/executors/codex/appServerConfig.ts` na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`            | _(edoghị)_                                                        | Token ikike/bearer etinyere ozugbo nke a na-egosi app-server. Ntụgharị maka connection ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerToken`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN_FILE`       | _(edoghị)_                                                        | Path gaa na faịlụ nwere token ikike app-server (site na `codex app-server --ws-token-file`). A na-eji ya mgbe edoghị `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`. Ntụgharị maka connection ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerTokenFile`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_CWD`                 | `/tmp`                                                            | Directory ọrụ ebe turn app-server na-agba. Ntụgharị maka connection ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerCwd`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_APPROVAL`            | _(edoghị)_                                                        | Iwu nkwado a na-enyefe turn app-server (dịka `never`, `on-request`). Ntụgharị maka connection ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerApprovalPolicy`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_SANDBOX`             | _(edoghị)_                                                        | Iwu sandbox a na-enyefe turn app-server (dịka `read-only`, `workspace-write`, `danger-full-access`). Mgbe edoghị ya, executor na-eji `workspace-write` dịka ndabara (e mere ka ọ sie ike; na mbụ ọ bụ `danger-full-access`). Ntụgharị maka connection ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerSandbox`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_AUTO_APPROVE`        | `false`                                                           | Kwado prompt nkwado nke app-server n’onwe ya na-akpaghị aka (mmezu command/faịlụ/ikike na host). A gbanyụrụ ya na ndabara — a na-ajụ prompt na-akpaghị aka; nke a anaghị emetụta oku tool harness (ha na-agafe n’ụzọ passthrough `item/tool/call` dị iche). Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`yes`. Ntụgharị maka connection ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerAutoApprove`.                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Oge iji nata header nzaghachi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000` (30s)                                                     | Oge nchere kachasị (ms) tupu nzaghachi amalite maka mbọ ọ bụla e mere ozugbo na-enweghị proxy. Mgbe oge gafere, a ga-anwale ya ọzọ otu ugboro site na socket ọhụrụ; tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ oke a ma jigide omume mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Oge iji nata body nzaghachi ahụ niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `30000`                                                           | Oge kacha elu maka ịmepụta njikọ TCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                    | `4000`                                                            | Oge kacha elu socket keep-alive ga-anọ n'enweghị ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Oge kacha elu maka proxy akara-mkpịsịaka TLS (wreq-js).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS`                    | `10000`                                                           | Na-amachi oge ruo mgbe byte mbụ rutere, kpọmkwem maka body nke transport akara-mkpịsịaka TLS nke wreq-js; naanị `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS` enweghị ike ịchọpụta body kwụsịrị ịga n'ihu n'ihi na ọ na-emecha ozugbo headers rutere (#12656). Mgbe oge gafere, ọ na-akagbu wreq reader ma laghachi na dispatcher ozugbo/proxy; `0` na-agbanyụọ watchdog ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `15000`                                                           | Maka OpenCode executor naanị, nakwa naanị mgbe feature flag `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_STALL_ROTATION` dị ọkụ (ọ na-adị gbanyụrụ na ndabara): na-amachi oge nchere maka byte mbụ nke body nke nzaghachi Responses a na-eziga dị ka stream mgbe headers ya rutere (#13484). Stream Responses na-eji `response.created` emepe, ya mere ịgbachi nkịtị gafee oge a bụ nkwụsị: a ga-eme ka akaụntụ ahụ zuru ike ma tụgharịa arịrịọ ahụ gaa n'akaụntụ na-esote otu ugboro; nkwụsị nke abụọ ga-eme ka ọ daa ozugbo. `0` na-agbanyụọ nchedo ahụ ọbụlagodi mgbe flag ahụ dị ọkụ. |
| `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `30000`                                                           | Oge kacha elu maka proxy hop nke arịrịọ bridge `/v1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `FIRECRAWL_BASE_URL`                            | `https://api.firecrawl.dev`                                       | Duziere Firecrawl web-fetch executor n'ebe instance nke ị na-akwado n'onwe gị dị (API key abụghị iwu mgbe ọ nọghị na cloud).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `FIRECRAWL_TIMEOUT_MS`                          | `30000`                                                           | Oge kacha elu maka arịrịọ ọ bụla nke Firecrawl web-fetch executor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `300000`                                                          | Oge kacha elu maka arịrịọ server niile site na bridge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`                                                           | Oge iji zipụ headers nzaghachi site na bridge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`        | `5000`                                                            | Oge kacha elu njikọ keep-alive nke bridge ga-anọ n'enweghị ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `0`                                                               | Oge kacha elu maka raw socket (0 = agbanyụrụ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_MS`                           | `30000`                                                           | Oge amara mgbe SIGTERM/SIGINT gasịrị tupu a manye ya ịpụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEFAULT_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `120000`                                                          | Uru ndabere nke `src/shared/utils/fetchTimeout.ts` na-eji mgbe edoghị `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_PROBE_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `8000`                                                            | Oge kacha elu (ms) maka presets `validationRead` na `modelsProbe` dị na `src/shared/network/safeOutboundFetch.ts`. Welie ya maka endpoints ndị na-adịghị ọsọ (Cerebras, Cloudflare AI, Groq) iji gbochie dashboard ịna-agbanwegharị n'etiti active/error. Ọ na-alaghachi na 8000ms maka uru na-ezighi ezi (<1000) ma ọ bụ uru na-abụghị ọnụọgụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`              | `25000`                                                           | Oge kacha elu fetch akọwapụtara maka relay na `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). Relay kwụsịrị ịrụ ọrụ ga-ada tupu oge kacha elu nke client/agent (~30s) ka ndị na-akpọ ya wee hụ ọdịda akọwapụtara maka relay kama ịhụ oge kacha elu upstream izugbe. A na-amachi ya na `29000` ka ọ na-eme mgbe niile tupu nke ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS`                    | `10`                                                              | Oge nkwụsị tupu nnwale ọzọ nke direct/relay/proxy retry-once paths na-ekerịta na `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). `0` = nwaa ọzọ ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLAUDE_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `60000`                                                           | Oge kacha elu maka arịrịọ wreq-js native (`claudeTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLAUDE_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Oge amara maka hard-deadline JS zuru oke nke agbakwunyere n'elu oge kacha elu native.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `30000`                                                           | Oge kacha elu maka arịrịọ wreq-js native (`perplexityTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Oge amara maka hard-deadline JS zuru oke nke agbakwunyere n'elu oge kacha elu native.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_SEARCH_HINT`                    | `0` (agbanyụrụ)                                                   | Na-agbakwụnye "Ị nwere ọchụchọ weebụ arụnyere n'ime ya. Zaa ajụjụ ozugbo site n'iji nsonaazụ ọchụchọ." na ozi sistemụ nke onye kpọrọ ya (`perplexity-web/protocol.ts`). A na-agbanyụ ya na ndabara — Perplexity ka na-eme ọchụchọ, ahịrịokwu ahụ na-apụtakwa na nzaghachi dịka nkọwa meta nye ndị ahịa ide koodu. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` iji weghachite ya.                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `60000`                                                           | Oge njedebe arịrịọ wreq-js nke native (`grokTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Oge mgbakwunye maka oke oge ikpeazụ JS zuru oke, nke a na-agbakwunye n'elu oge njedebe native.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                                                           | Oge njedebe arịrịọ wreq-js nke native (`notionTlsClient.ts`); `notion-web` na-ebuli ya kwa arịrịọ ruo `180000` maka mmepụta na-ewe ogologo oge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Oge mgbakwunye maka oke oge ikpeazụ JS zuru oke, nke a na-agbakwunye n'elu oge njedebe native.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_BROWSER_POOL`                        | `on`                                                              | Ọdọ browser Playwright a na-ekekọrịta maka nkata kuki-weebụ nke browser na-akwado (`browserPool.ts`); tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `off` iji gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OBSCURA_BIN`                                   | `auto-detect`                                                     | Ụzọ gaa na binary `obscura` nke ọdọ browser na onye mmebe Cloudflare Playground ji dịka injin bụ isi (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`); a na-achọpụta ya na-akpaghị aka site na PATH nke sistemụ mgbe a na-edoghị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OBSCURA_CDP_ENDPOINT`                          | _(edoghị ya)_                                                     | Tụọ aka na Obscura na-agba ọsọ ugbua (`http://host:port`) kama ịmalite nke ọhụrụ; modul ahụ anaghị achịkwa usoro ahụ (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OBSCURA_PORT`                                  | `random free port`                                                | Port akọwapụtara maka `obscura serve` a malitere; a na-ahọrọ port efu na-akpaghị aka mgbe a na-edoghị ya (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `WEB_COOKIE_USE_BROWSER`                        | `0`                                                               | Tinye arịrịọ nkata kuki-weebụ n'ụzọ nke browser na-akwado (`browserBackedChat.ts`); jiri `1` mee ka ọ rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL`                             | `https://www.kimi.ai`                                             | URL ntọala maka onye mmebe Kimi Web (kimi.ai Connect-RPC mba ụwa) (`kimi-web.ts`); gbanwee ya naanị maka endpoint enyo/proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `KIMI_WEB_CHAT_URL`                             | `<KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL>/apiv2/kimi.gateway.chat.v1.ChatService/Chat` | Endpoint nkata zuru ezu maka onye mmebe Kimi Web (`kimi-web.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | _(a na-achọpụta ya na-akpaghị aka)_                               | Ụzọ gaa na executable Chrome/Edge nke sistemụ maka nbanye browser mmekọrịta Adobe Firefly (`adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`); ọ na-anọchi nchọpụta akpaka nke OS ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                      | _mmepụta standalone .build/_                                      | Mgbanwe n'oge build maka ndekọ mmepụta standalone nke usoro idokọta ihe ọnụ mgbe build gachara na-eji (`scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`); ọ bụ maka ngwa build, ọ bụghị runtime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

Mgbalị target combo na-eketa oge njedebe arịrịọ upstream ekpebiri (`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`, ma ọ bụ
`REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` mgbe ọ na-enye ndabara fetch). Tọọ `targetTimeoutMs` n'ime combo,
ndabara combo, ma ọ bụ override provider naanị iji mee ka fallback combo dị ngwa; a na-amachi uru karịrị
oge njedebe upstream dị ugbu a ka ọ bụrụ oge njedebe upstream ahụ.

`comboTimeoutMs` bụ mmefu oge wall-clock dị iche maka combo dum n'ofe target failover.
Hapụ ya ka edoghị ya ma ọ bụ tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji debe iteration na-enweghị oke (ihe nkwụsị maka ịrapara nke nkeji 10
`COMBO_LOOP_SAFETY_TIMEOUT_MS` ka na-emetụta). Uru dị mma na-anọchi safety net ahụ
maka combo. Debe `comboTimeoutMs` ka ọ dị ogologo karịa `targetTimeoutMs` ka failover
ka nwee oge mgbe target mbụ dị nwayọ.

### Oke Circuit Breaker

Nhazi circuit breaker n'ọkwa provider. Ndabara ndị a na-egosipụta uru e mere ka ha bawanye nke a na-eji kemgbe v3.6 maka njikọ 500+.

| Agbanwe                                                       | Ndabara   | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite              | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_THRESHOLD`                   | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Ọnụ ọgụgụ ọdịda ndị na-esochi ibe ha maka ndị na-eweta OAuth tupu circuit breaker ahụ amalite ọrụ.                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_RESET_MS`                    | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Oge windo ntọgharị (ms) maka circuit breaker nke onye na-eweta OAuth.                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_THRESHOLD`                 | `12`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Ọnụ ọgụgụ ọdịda ndị na-esochi ibe ha maka ndị na-eweta API-key.                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_RESET_MS`                  | `30000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Oge windo ntọgharị (ms) maka circuit breaker nke onye na-eweta API-key.                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_THRESHOLD`                   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Ọnụ ọgụgụ ọdịda ndị na-esochi ibe ha maka ndị na-eweta mpaghara (Ollama, LM Studio, ...).                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_RESET_MS`                    | `15000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Oge windo ntọgharị (ms) maka circuit breaker nke onye na-eweta mpaghara.                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `10`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Circuit breaker ọkwa onye na-eweta: ọdịda dị n'ime windo tupu onye na-eweta OAuth niile abanye n'oge nchere.                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `900000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Circuit breaker ọkwa onye na-eweta: windo ngụkọ ọdịda na-aga n'ihu (ms) maka ndị na-eweta OAuth.                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Circuit breaker ọkwa onye na-eweta: oge nchere (ms) ozugbo onye na-eweta OAuth ruru oke ahụ.                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`      | `5`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Onye na-eweta OAuth na-abanye na DEGRADED mgbe ọdịda ruru ọnụ ọgụgụ a.                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`     | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Ọnụ ọgụgụ kachasị elu e ji amụba nrịgo `resetTimeout` nke onye na-eweta OAuth.                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT`   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Onye na-eweta OAuth na-ebuli ọkwa mgbe okirikiri mmeghe ruru ọnụ ọgụgụ a.                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`        | `15`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Circuit breaker ọkwa onye na-eweta: ọdịda dị n'ime windo tupu onye na-eweta API-key niile abanye n'oge nchere.                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`        | `1800000` | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Circuit breaker ọkwa onye na-eweta: windo ngụkọ ọdịda na-aga n'ihu (ms) maka ndị na-eweta API-key.                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`              | `600000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Circuit breaker ọkwa onye na-eweta: oge nchere (ms) ozugbo onye na-eweta API-key ruru oke ahụ.                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`    | `7`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Onye na-eweta API-key na-abanye na DEGRADED mgbe ọdịda ruru ọnụ ọgụgụ a.                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`   | `4`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Ọnụ ọgụgụ kachasị elu e ji amụba nrịgo `resetTimeout` nke onye na-eweta API-key.                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT` | `3`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Onye na-eweta API-key na-ebuli ọkwa mgbe okirikiri mmeghe ruru ọnụ ọgụgụ a.                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Circuit breaker ọkwa onye na-eweta: ọdịda tupu onye na-eweta mpaghara niile abanye n'oge nchere.                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Circuit breaker ọkwa onye na-eweta: windo ngụkọ ọdịda na-aga n'ihu (ms) maka ndị na-eweta mpaghara.                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Circuit breaker ọkwa onye na-eweta: oge nchere (ms) ozugbo onye na-eweta mpaghara ruru oke ahụ.                                                                     |
| `PIN_DROP_BACKOFF_LEVEL`                                      | `2`       | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`   | Omimi nkwụsịlaghachi ebe a na-ewere onye na-eweta pin nke cache ọnọdụ dị ka onye na-adịghị mma ogologo oge, wee wepụ pin ahụ iji gafee na onye ọzọ ma e nwee ọdịda. |
| `PIN_DROP_GRACE_MS`                                           | `20000`   | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`   | Windo mgbochi mgbanwe ugboro ugboro (ms) nke na-anabata oge nchere nwa oge dị mkpirikpi tupu e wepụ pin nke cache ọnọdụ.                                            |

### Ọnọdụ

| Ọnọdụ                                   | Nhazi                                                    |
| --------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Mmepụta koodu na-ewe ogologo oge**    | `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS=900000` (nkeji 15)                   |
| **Kpaara ngụkọta oge stream ga-adị**    | `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS=1260000` (nkeji 21)            |
| **Ọdịda ngwa ngwa maka API mmepụta**    | `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS=10000`                      |
| **Ụdị ndị na-eche echiche ogologo oge** | `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS=300000` (nkeji 5 n'etiti chunks) |

---

## 16. Ndekọ

Sistemụ ndekọ na-ede ma na stdout ma na faịlụ ndekọ ndị a na-atụgharị. A na-agụ nhazi niile site na `src/lib/logEnv.ts`.

| Mgbanwe                                   | Ndabara                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ----------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `APP_LOG_LEVEL`                           | `info`                     | Ọkwa ndekọ kacha nta: `debug`, `info`, `warn`, `error`.                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `APP_LOG_FORMAT`                          | `text`                     | Ọdịdị mmepụta: `text` (nke mmadụ nwere ike ịgụ) ma ọ bụ `json` (ahaziri ahazi).                                                                                                                                                            |
| `APP_LOG_TO_FILE`                         | `true`                     | Dee ndekọ na faịlụ yana stdout.                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `APP_LOG_FILE_PATH`                       | `logs/application/app.log` | Ụzọ faịlụ ndekọ (nke metụtara mgbọrọgwụ ọrụ ahụ ma ọ bụ `DATA_DIR`).                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILE_SIZE`                   | `50M`                      | Ogo faịlụ kachasị tupu ntụgharị. Na-anabata: `50M`, `1G`, `512K`, ma ọ bụ bytes nkịtị.                                                                                                                                                     |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                  | `7`                        | Ụbọchị ole a ga-edobe faịlụ ndekọ ngwa ndị a tụgharịworo.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILES`                       | `20`                       | Ọnụ ọgụgụ kachasị nke ndabere faịlụ ndekọ ndị a tụgharịworo.                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                 | `7`                        | Ụbọchị ole a ga-edobe ndenye ndekọ arịrịọ/ọkpụkpọ na ọdụ data.                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CALL_LOG_MAX_ENTRIES`                    | `10000`                    | Ọnụ ọgụgụ kachasị nke ndenye ndekọ ọkpụkpọ na nchekwa nwa oge dị na ebe nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CALL_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`                | `100000`                   | Ahịrị kachasị na tebụl SQLite `call_logs` tupu ihichapụ ndị ochie.                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ENABLE_REQUEST_LOGS`                     | _(edoghị)_                 | Manye ndekọ arịrịọ zuru ezu ka ọ rụọ ọrụ ma ọ bụ kwụsị, na-akagbu ntọala dashboard.                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MAX_PENDING_REQUEST_AGE_MS`              | `3600000` (1 elekere)      | Oge kachasị maka ndenye ndekọ arịrịọ na-arụ ọrụ ma enweghị onye nwe ya tupu nhichapụ n'ime ebe nchekwa.                                                                                                                                    |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_CAPTURE_STREAM_CHUNKS` | `false`                    | Chekwaa iberibe stream n'ime ihe pipeline mgbe `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`. Nhọrọ mmadụ ga-agbanye (`true`) — agbanyụghị ya na ndabara iji chekwaa diski.                                                                             |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_MAX_SIZE_KB`           | `512`                      | Ogo kachasị nke ihe ndekọ ọkpụkpọ pipeline na KB mgbe `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PROXY_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`               | `100000`                   | Ahịrị kachasị na tebụl SQLite `proxy_logs` tupu ihichapụ ndị ochie.                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                   | `false`                    | Tinye IP ndị ahịa/ọpụpụ na prefix akaụntụ n'ime ndekọ console `[ProxyEgress]`. Ndekọ proxy-log dị na dashboard/ọdụ data na-edobe nkọwa niile.                                                                                              |
| `APP_LOG_ROTATION_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS`      | `60000` (1 nkeji)          | Ugboro ole `src/lib/logRotation.ts` na-enyocha ọzọ ogo faịlụ ndekọ na-arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CHAT_LOG_TEXT_LIMIT`                     | `65536`                    | Ogologo eriri mkpụrụedemede kachasị a na-edobe n'ime ihe ndekọ nkata (ndabara bụ 64 KB).                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CHAT_LOG_ARRAY_TAIL_ITEMS`               | `128`                      | Ọnụ ọgụgụ ihe array ndị a na-edobe site na ngwụcha mgbe a na-ebelata payload ndekọ nkata.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_DEPTH`                      | `6`                        | Omimi ntinye kachasị tupu ebelata payload ndekọ nkata.                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_OBJECT_KEYS`                | `80`                       | Ọnụ ọgụgụ key object kachasị a na-edobe na payload ndekọ nkata (0 = enweghị oke).                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_BODY_KB`                    | `1024`                     | Ogo body arịrịọ/nzaghachi dum (KB) nke, mgbe ọ gafere ya, a ga-eji nchịkọta nkịtị dochie ya kama clone zuru ezu. Bulie nke a ma ọ bụrụ na mkparịta ụka agentic ndị toro ogologo na-egosi placeholder kama ozi ndị ahụ n'ezie na dashboard. |
| `CHAT_DEBUG_FILE`                         | `false`                    | Mgbe ọ bụ true, `serializeArtifactForStorage` na-awụpụ mbelata dabere na nha. Maka debug naanị.                                                                                                                                            |
