## 17. Nkwalite Ojiji Ebe Nchekwa

| Mgbanwe                       | Ndabara                                                   | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB`         | _akpakaaka_ (ígwè nkịtị); **`1024` n'ime onyonyo Docker** | Oke heap V8 (MB) **a tụrụ aro** maka Docker/ọrụ kwụụrụ onwe ya. Mgbe edoghị ya, a na-ahazi ya n'ụzọ dinamiki (~35% nke RAM sistemụ, ma debe ya n'agbata `[512, 4096]`); `512` bụ naanị oke kacha nta mgbe enweghị ike ịgụ ebe nchekwa niile. Na `run-standalone.mjs` (Docker CMD), a na-agbakwunye uru **e depụtara kpọmkwem** dịka `--max-old-space-size`, ọ na-enwekwa **mmeri** karịa ọkọlọtọ heap NODE_OPTIONS na-emegide ya (ọkọlọtọ ikpeazụ nke V8). `omniroute serve` ka na-ahọrọ ọkọlọtọ heap NODE_OPTIONS dị adị (#5238). Edoghị ha abụọ ka ha bụrụ ọnụọgụ dị iche — usoro ahụ na-edekọ ịdọ aka ná ntị nke na-akpọ uru abụọ ahụ na nke meriri. **Onyonyo Docker gọọmentị na-edobe `1024` mgbe niile, ya mere nhazi akpaka anaghị arụ ọrụ n'ebe ahụ.** Coding-agent `/v1/responses` chọrọ `8192`–`12288` tinyere ohere mgbakwunye cgroup — lee [Ntuziaka Docker — RAM oge ọrụ](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#runtime-ram-for-coding-agents). |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`       | `50`                                                      | Ọnụọgụ kacha elu nke ndenye prompt sistemụ echekwara na cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`      | `2097152` (2 MB)                                          | Oke nha mkpokọta cache prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_TTL_MS`         | `300000` (nkeji 5)                                        | TTL nke ndenye cache prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`     | `100`                                                     | Ọnụọgụ kacha elu nke nzaghachi temperature=0 echekwara na cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`    | `4194304` (4 MB)                                          | Oke nha mkpokọta cache semantiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_TTL_MS`       | `1800000` (nkeji 30)                                      | TTL nke ndenye cache semantiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE` | `5`                                                       | Mgbọrọgwụ corpus mpaghara ndị na-edobe ndeksi dị ndụ n'ime ebe nchekwa n'otu oge (`src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`). LRU: mgbe e ruru oke ahụ, a na-ewepụ ndeksi mgbọrọgwụ ejirighị eme ihe kemgbe ogologo oge, a ga-ewughachikwa ya mgbe a jụrụ ya ọzọ. A na-amachi ya ka ọ ghara ịdị n'okpuru `1`; uru na-abụghị ọnụọgụ na-alaghachi na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `STREAM_HISTORY_MAX`          | `50`                                                      | Ọnụọgụ kacha elu nke mmemme stream na-adịbeghị anya n'ime buffer nlele dị ndụ nke Dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT`      | `128000`                                                  | Oke ogologo context ndabere zuru ụwa ọnụ maka model ndị na-enweghị nhazi akọwapụtara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `USAGE_TOKEN_BUFFER`          | `100`                                                     | Oghere token mgbakwunye edobere mgbe a na-enyocha oke ojiji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

### Mkpakọ

| Mgbanwe                               | Ndabara | Nkọwa                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_RTK_TRUST_PROJECT_FILTERS` | edoghị  | Tụkwasị `.rtk/filters.json` nke project obi n'enweghị hash `.rtk/trust.json`. Jiri naanị na mmepe mpaghara a na-achịkwa. |

### Injin Ebe Nchekwa (atụmatụ 21)

### Ọnụ ahịa event-loop nke ebe nchekwa, nka, na mmegharị token (#10349)

OmniRoute bụ **otu usoro Node**. Mwepụta/nweghachi ebe nchekwa, ntinye nka, na mmegharị token provider na-arụ ọrụ n'otu **event loop ahụ** dịka `GET /healthz` na dashboard. Ha abụghị worker thread.

| Ọrụ                               | Koodu                              | Ndabara                                                | Njikwa onye ọrụ                                                                                                                          |
| --------------------------------- | ---------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Mwepụta / iweghachite ebe nchekwa | `src/lib/memory/`                  | **memoryEnabled** na Dashboard (na-agbanye na ndabara) | Gbanyụọ **Settings → Memory**. Enweghị env kill switch dị iche karịa ịgbanyụọ atụmatụ ahụ na ntọala.                                     |
| Ntinye skills                     | `src/lib/skills/injection.ts`      | **skillsEnabled** na Dashboard (na-agbanye na ndabara) | Gbanyụọ **Settings → Memory/Skills** (`skillsEnabled`). Nhọrọ sandbox ndị dị n'okpuru na-amachi naanị mmezu mgbe agbanyelarị ntinye ahụ. |
| Ime token ka ọ dị ọhụrụ           | `src/sse/services/tokenRefresh.ts` | Na-agbanye maka ndị na-eweta OAuth/web ejikọrọ         | Kwụpụ onye na-eweta ahụ ma ọ bụ hapụ token ka ha nọgide na-adị irè; enweghị env `TOKEN_REFRESH=0` taa.                                   |

Ọ bụrụ na `/healthz` na-egbu oge n'igbe na-enweghị nnukwu ọrụ, buru ụzọ gbanyụọ memory + skills, wee lelee ibu catalog/compression (#10303, #9685). Atụmatụ ndị a na-enye ohere na ebe `await`, mana ha ka na-asọrịta mpi maka otu thread ahụ.

Nhọrọ nhazi maka oyi akwa embedding, vector store na reranking nke sistemụ ebe nchekwa na-adịgide adịgide (`src/lib/memory/`).

| Variable                             | Ndabara                     | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_TTL_MS`      | `300000` (nkeji 5)          | TTL maka cache embedding dị na memory (maka akara source/model/dim ọ bụla).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_MAX`         | `1000`                      | Ọnụọgụ kachasị elu nke ndenye LRU a na-edebe na cache embedding.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `MEMORY_TRANSFORMERS_MODEL`          | `Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2`   | HF repo id maka pipeline MiniLM mpaghara `@huggingface/transformers` nke a na-ahọrọ ịgbanye (~23 MB int8, ~400 MB RAM).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_MODEL`                | `minishlab/potion-base-8M`  | HF repo id maka embedder static potion/Model2Vec nke dabere na tebụl ọchụchọ. A na-ebudata ya nwayọ nwayọ n'ime cache dir mgbe achọrọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_CACHE_DIR`            | `<DATA_DIR>/embeddings`     | Directory eji echekwa faịlụ model static potion na cache. Mgbe edoghị ya, ndabara ya dị n'okpuru `DATA_DIR`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `HF_HUB_ENDPOINT`                    | `https://huggingface.co`    | Dochie base URL nke Hugging Face Hub nke `staticPotion.ts` na-eji (dịka endpoint mirror maka nhazi air-gapped).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `MEMORY_VEC_TOP_K`                   | `20`                        | top-K ndabara nke ọchụchọ vector brute-force `sqlite-vec` na-eji n'ime `src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MEMORY_RRF_K`                       | `60`                        | Constant `k` nke Reciprocal Rank Fusion maka iweghachite ngwakọta FTS5 + vector (usoro sqlite-vec).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `VECTOR_STORE_DISABLE_VEC`           | `false`                     | Ebe nnwale/nchọpụta nsogbu na `getVectorStore()` (`src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`): mgbe ọ bụ `true`, ọ na-amanye vector store ka ọ bụrụ `null` (na-eme ka gburugburu cloud/WASM na-enweghị `sqlite-vec`), nke na-eweda iweghachite memory gaa na ọchụchọ keyword FTS5. Hapụ ya n'enweghị ntọala na production.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `NOTION_API_KEY`                     | _(edoghị ya)_               | API key maka backend Notion (preset backend amaara nke `genericBackend.ts` na-eji).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `NOTION_API_URL`                     | `https://api.notion.com/v1` | Base URL maka Notion API (enwere ike dochie ya maka nhọrọ Notion a na-akwado n'onwe gị).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_KEY`                   | _(edoghị ya)_               | API key maka backend Obsidian Vault (preset backend amaara nke `genericBackend.ts` na-eji).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_URL`                   | `http://localhost:27123`    | Base URL maka Obsidian Vault API (enwere ike dochie ya maka vault dị anya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED`         | `false`                     | Mgbanwe ukwu maka mbelata typed memory nke TV6. **A ga-ahọrọ ịgbanye ya (ndabara agbanyụrụ)** — sweep ahụ **na-ehichapụ** memory ndị rere ure. Mgbe agbanyụrụ ya, `access_count`/`last_accessed_at` bụ naanị telemetry, ọ dịghịkwa ihe a na-ehichapụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_EPISODIC_DAYS`   | `30`                        | TTL (ụbọchị) nke mgbe ọ gafesịrị memory `episodic` a na-ejighị eme ihe ga-ere ure. `0` na-emekwa ka episodic ghara ire ure. Ụdị na-adịgide adịgide (`factual`/`procedural`/`semantic`) anaghị ere ure mgbe niile. Elekere decay na-amalitegharị dabere na `last_accessed_at`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ACCESS_IMMUNITY` | `3`                         | Memory etinyere ugboro `>=` ọnụọgụ a agaghịzi ere ure n'agbanyeghị ụdị ya. `0` na-agbanyụọ nchedo sitere na access.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_SWEEP_INTERVAL`  | `0` (agbanyụrụ)             | Oge etiti (sekọnd) maka sweep decay oge ụfọdụ nke nhọrọ dị na `src/lib/memory/typedDecay.ts`. `0`/edoghị ya = enweghị sweep oge ụfọdụ. A ga-ahọrọ ịgbanye ya n'ụzọ abụọ: ọ chọkwara `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRICT_SYSTEM_PROVIDERS`  | _(edoghị ya)_               | Provider ids ndị e ji rikoma kewaa (anaghị ele nnukwu/obere mkpụrụedemede anya) nke na-anabata ozi `system` **naanị na index 0** (`src/lib/memory/injection.ts`). Maka ndị a, splice memory etiti-array nke na-echekwa cache adịghị mma na mkparịta ụka multi-turn, ya mere a na-ejikọta/etinye memory n'ihu dịka ozi system mbụ. Ndabara bụ naanị `xiaomi-mimo`/`mimo`; gbasaa ya maka endpoint OpenAI-compatible a na-akwado n'onwe gị (dịka Qwen3.5+/3.6) nke chat template ya na-amanye otu mgbochi ahụ nke naanị otu ozi system dị n'isi. |

### Ihe Nlereanya Docker nwere RAM Dị Ala

`128` bụ naanị maka dashboard. Coding agents nọ na heap a na-enweta `FATAL ERROR` n'oge `/v1/responses` dị ogologo. Ejila ihe nlereanya a dịka gateway Claude/Codex/Grok.

```bash
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=128
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=20
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=524288        # 512 KB
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=25
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=1048576     # 1 MB
STREAM_HISTORY_MAX=10
```

---

## 18. Mmekọrịta Ọnụahịa

Mmekọrịta data ọnụahịa ụdịdị na-akpaghị aka site na isi mmalite mpụga.

| Mgbanwe                 | Ndabara       | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta        | Nkọwa                                            |
| ----------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `PRICING_SYNC_ENABLED`  | `false`       | `src/lib/pricingSync.ts` | Mmekọrịta ọnụahịa oge ọbụla a na-ahọrọ ịgbalite. |
| `PRICING_SYNC_INTERVAL` | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/pricingSync.ts` | Oge dị n'etiti mmekọrịta na sekọnd.              |
| `PRICING_SYNC_SOURCES`  | `litellm`     | `src/lib/pricingSync.ts` | Isi mmalite data ndị rikoma kewara.              |

---

## Mmekọrịta Arena ELO

| Mgbanwe                      | Ndabara       | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta                                                                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ---------------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ARENA_ELO_SYNC_ENABLED`     | `true`        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                     | Mmekọrịta ELO nke bọọdụ ndị-isi Arena AI n'oge ụfọdụ, nke a pụrụ ịhazi site na Dashboard Feature Flags ma ọ bụ jiri `false` kwụsị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `MODELS_CATALOG_PREFIX_MODE` | `dual`        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`, `src/app/api/v1/models/catalog.ts` | Ụdị prefix a na-eji maka id ụdịdị na `GET /v1/models`. `dual` na-akpọsa ma prefix alias dị mkpụmkpụ ma prefix provider canonical maka ụdịdị ọ bụla (ndakọrịta azụ — ọ fọrọ nke nta ka ọ bụrụ na ọ na-eme katalọgụ ahụ okpukpu abụọ); `alias` na-ewepụta otu id maka ụdịdị ọ bụla; `canonical` na-ewepụta naanị prefix provider-id zuru ezu (providers nke alias ha bụrịrị id canonical na-edobe otu ndenye ha). Clients nwere ike iji `?prefix=alias` gbanwee ya maka arịrịọ ọ bụla. Lee [API_REFERENCE](API_REFERENCE.md#model-id-prefixes-prefix). |
| `ARENA_ELO_SYNC_INTERVAL`    | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/arenaEloSync.ts`                                                            | Oge dị n'etiti mmekọrịta na sekọnd.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

---

## Provider PromptQL Playground (Na-abụghị nke Gọọmentị/Nnwale)

Njikọ nnọkọ GraphQL e ji reverse engineering mepụta maka prompt.ql.app (`src/shared/constants/providers/web-cookie.ts`). Ha niile bụ nhọrọ — ndabara na-arụtụ aka na endpoints playground ọha; gbanwee naanị maka deployment PromptQL nke ị na-akwado n'onwe gị/ọzọ.

| Mgbanwe                      | Ndabara                                                            | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta                                                       | Nkọwa                                                          |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROMPTQL_GRAPHQL_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.prompt.ql.app/promptql/playground-v2-hge/v1/graphql` | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Endpoint GraphQL e ji arụ ọrụ nkata/nnọkọ.                     |
| `PROMPTQL_CREDITS_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.pro.ql.app/v1/graphql`                               | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`, `open-sse/services/usage/promptql.ts` | Endpoint GraphQL e ji ajụ banyere itule kredit/ojiji.          |
| `PROMPTQL_TOKEN_REFRESH_URL` | `https://auth.pro.ql.app/ddn/project/token`                        | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Endpoint e ji anwa ime ka token dị ọhụrụ.                      |
| `PROMPTQL_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `180000`                                                           | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Oge kachasị (ms) iji na-enyocha `thread_events` tupu oge agwụ. |

## Onye Na-eweta Weebụ HyperAgent (Nke Na-abụghị nke Gọọmenti/Nnwale)

Àkwà mmiri nnọkọ e mepụtara site na reverse engineering maka hyperagent.com (`src/shared/constants/providers/web-cookie.ts`). Ọ bụ nhọrọ — ndabara na-atụ aka na endpoint ịgba ụgwọ/ojiji ọha; gbanwee ya naanị maka nrụnye HyperAgent nke ị na-akwado n'onwe gị ma ọ bụ nrụnye ọzọ.

| Ngbanwe                | Ndabara                                             | Faịlụ Isi                               | Nkọwa                                            |
| ---------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `HYPERAGENT_USAGE_URL` | `https://hyperagent.com/api/settings/billing/usage` | `open-sse/services/usage/hyperagent.ts` | Endpoint eji enweta blọk kredit ịgba ụgwọ/ojiji. |

## Oke Ojiji Kilo Code

Nnyocha ego USD nkeonwe na ojiji Kilo Pass maka onye na-eweta Kilo Code. Ọ bụ nhọrọ — ndabara na-atụ aka na Kilo API ọha; gbanwee ya naanị maka relay/ngwa nnwale. Nkwenye njirimara na-eji token nnweta OAuth dị ugbu a nke njikọ ahụ.

| Ngbanwe        | Ndabara               | Faịlụ Isi                             | Nkọwa                                                           |
| -------------- | --------------------- | ------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KILO_API_URL` | `https://api.kilo.ai` | `open-sse/services/usage/kilocode.ts` | URL ntọala eji enweta ego Kilo Code nkeonwe na ojiji Kilo Pass. |

## Onye Na-eweta Weebụ Adobe Firefly (Nke Na-abụghị nke Gọọmenti/Nnwale)

Mmegharị nnọkọ nke ihe nchọgharị na-achịkwa maka onye na-eweta weebụ Adobe Firefly
(`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`, `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`,
`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`). Ọ bụ nhọrọ — a haziri ndabara niile maka nrụnye
desktọpụ nkịtị.

> **E wepụrụ ya na #9255.** E ji ọrụ nbanye ihe nchọgharị Playwright dochie sistemụ Chrome ochie e jikọrọ na CDP (adobeFireflyChromeRuntime.ts), ya mere ntọala njikwa ya adịghịzi. Ngbanwe
> ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_ CDP_PORT / VISIBLE / HEADED / PING / FORCE_RESTART, tinyere
> ADOBE_FIREFLY_LOGIN_WAIT_MS na ADOBE_FIREFLY_FORTER_WAIT_MS, anaghị agụ ebe ọ bụla n'ime
> codebase — ịtọ ha enweghị mmetụta ọ bụla.

| Ngbanwe                              | Ndabara    | Faịlụ Isi                                       | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------ | ---------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`      | `0`        | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` maka Chrome headless n'ezie (a maara na ọ naghị arụ ọrụ maka generate; maka debugging naanị).                         |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`      | `1`        | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Ịhọrọ ịgbanye/gbanyụọ ikpo ihe nchọgharị ọkụ tupu oge eruo. `0` na-akwụsị ikpo ọkụ tupu oge eruo (mgbake 408 n'etiti batch ka na-arụ ọrụ). |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`         | `1`        | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji kwụsị ichekwa nnọkọ Adobe Firefly na diski.                                                                       |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`    | _(edoghị)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Oghere kacha nta (ms) a na-amanye n'etiti submit ndị na-esochi ibe ha, nke na-anọchi ndabara arụnyere.                                     |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`   | _(edoghị)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Oghere mgbakwunye (ms) a na-etinye mgbe batch gara nke ọma, nke na-anọchi ndabara arụnyere.                                                |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS` | _(edoghị)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`       | Oge nchere ntọala (ms) tupu izipu arịrịọ generation, nke na-anọchi ndabara arụnyere.                                                       |

## 19. Mmekọrịta Model (Dev)

| Mgbanwe                             | Ndabara       | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                  | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ----------------------------------- | ------------- | ---------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_ENABLED`           | _(edobeghị)_  | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Nchịkwa siri ike maka mmekọrịta ọnụahịa models.dev. Ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya = soro Ntọala > AI (`modelsDevSyncEnabled`). `0`/`false`/`off`/`no` **na-emeri DB** ma na-amafere ma mmekọrịta oge niile ma nyocha SQL/JSON nke `getModelsDevPricing()` (mgbake mgbe dashboard rapaara n'otu event loop ahụ). `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes` na-amanye ka mmekọrịta rụọ ọrụ. Nchekwa/nhichapụ ọnụahịa ka na-akpọ `backupDbFile("pre-write")`, nke anaghị eme ihe n'okpuru mmachi nke nkeji 60 ma ọ bụ `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`. |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_INTERVAL`          | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Ogologo oge n'etiti mmekọrịta katalọgụ model n'oge mmepe, na sekọnd.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL` | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/contextWindowResolver.ts` | Ogologo oge (na sekọnd) maka ihe mmezi context-window nke na-emezi onwe ya (5004): ọ na-edobe windo ndị provider kwupụtara site na nchọpụta `/models` dịka mgbanwe `auto:discovery` mgbe ha dị iche na katalọgụ. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya. Ọ na-eji data emekọrịtalarị ọzọ (enweghị fetch ọhụrụ); ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-edegharị mgbanwe `manual`.                                                                                                                                                            |

---

`ENABLE_CC_COMPATIBLE_PROVIDER` bụ naanị maka relays nke ndị ọzọ na-ahụ maka nke na-anabata naanị ndị ahịa Claude Code. OmniRoute na-edegharị arịrịọ ka relays ndị ahụ nwee ike ịnabata ha. Ọ bụrụ na naanị ihe ị chọrọ bụ iji Claude Code CLI, ma ọ bụ na ị maghị nke ọma ihe relays ndị a bụ, hapụ nke a ka ọ bụrụ ihe agbanyụghị ma tinye provider nkịtị dakọtara na Anthropic kama.
