## 21. Ahụike Proxy

| Variable                                          | Ndabara                  | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                                            | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROXY_FAST_FAIL_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `2000`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                     | Oge ngwụcha maka nyocha ahụike ọdịda-ngwa-ngwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_LATENCY_WINDOW_HOURS`                      | `3`                      | `src/lib/db/proxies.ts`                                                                      | Oghere oge (n'ime awa) maka ịgbakọ nkezi latency nke proxy ndị a na-atụle n'ime usoro pool ahaziri maka latency.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS`                       | `30000`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                     | TTL nke cache maka nsonaazụ nyocha ahụike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_UNHEALTHY_CACHE_TTL_MS`             | `2000`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                     | TTL nke cache maka nyocha ahụike proxy ndị dara. Mee ka nke a dị mkpụmkpụ karịa `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS` ka e wee nwalee ọzọ ngwa ngwa mgbe oge proxy gafere n'ihi ọtụtụ arịrịọ na-eme n'otu oge, n'emeghị ka fast-fail kwụsị ịrụ ọrụ maka proxy ndị nwụrụ anwụ n'ezie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_ENABLED`                            | `true`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                           | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji gbanyụọ scheduler ahụike proxy nke na-arụ ọrụ n'azụ ma na-enyocha proxy ndị e debanyere aha site n'oge ruo n'oge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_INTERVAL_MS`                        | `600000`                 | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                           | Oghere oge n'etiti nyocha zuru ezu nke scheduler ahụike na-arụ n'azụ, n'ime ms (opekempe `60000`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_URL`                           | `https://httpbin.org/ip` | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                         | Ebumnuche nyocha nnweta nke scheduler na endpoint `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test` na-eji. Tụnye ya aka na URL dị n'ime/nke ị na-akwado n'onwe gị iji zere ndabara ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_CONCURRENCY`                   | `10`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                         | Ọnụọgụ nyocha a na-amalite n'otu oge n'ime batch ọ bụla, nke scheduler na endpoint `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test` na-ekekọrịta. A na-edobe opekempe ya na 1 ma kpachie ya na 50.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_STAGGER_MS`                    | `100`                    | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                         | Oge nchere na ms n'etiti ọpụpụ nyocha abụọ n'ime otu batch. Na-enweghị ya, batch ahụ dum ga-apụ n'otu oge, IP ọpụpụ ha na-ekekọrịta nwekwara ike ime ka ebumnuche nwere mmachi ọsọ kwụsị ha. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ nkewa oge ahụ; a kpachiri ya na 5000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_USE_PROVIDER_TARGET`                | `true`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth/providerProbeTarget.ts`                                                 | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ "false" iji kwụsị inyocha ezigbo host nke provider e kenyere proxy (`GET /models`, enweghị API key) ma jiri `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_URL` mgbe niile kama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_AUTO_DEACTIVATE`                    | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/statusPolicy.ts`                                                        | Mgbe ọ bụ `false` (ndabara), nyocha nnweta akpaaka (scheduler + bọtịnụ "Nwalee Ha Niile" nke `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test`) bụ **naanị maka ọgụgụ** ma ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-ede ọnọdụ proxy — naanị onye njikwa na-ahọpụta active/inactive, ka nyocha na-adịghị akwụsi ike ghara ịhapụ proxy e kenyere na-enweghị ọrụ (#6246). Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji weghachite omume ochie nke inwale-ma-tọọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PROXY_POOL_EGRESS_OBSERVATION`                   | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                           | Akara njirimara a na-ahọrọ iji gbanye (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ntọala dochie ya na DB dashboard ga-emeri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-egosi nlele ọpụpụ pool nke bụ naanị maka ọgụgụ n'okpuru proxy pool dị na dashboard (IP ọpụpụ dị iche iche, njikọ, na nke a kacha hụ n'azụ otu IP n'ime awa 24 gara aga, sitere na ndekọ proxy). Ọ dịghị mgbe a na-eji ya maka ịhọrọ ụzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE`                               | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                           | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji kwe ka scheduler wepụ proxy na-akpaghị aka mgbe ọdịda ugboro ugboro n'usoro gasịrị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER`                         | `3`                      | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                           | Ọnụọgụ ọdịda na-esochi onwe ha tupu scheduler ewepụ proxy na-akpaghị aka (mgbe `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE=true`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROXY_AUTO_DISABLE`                              | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                           | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji kwe ka scheduler jiri nwayọọ gbanyụọ proxy (ọnọdụ `dead`, anaghị ehichapụ ya) mgbe ọdịda ndị na-esochi onwe ha mere ugboro ugboro, kama iwepụ ya. Ọ bụ nhọrọ na-adịghị ebibi ihe maka `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE`: proxy ahụ na-apụ ozugbo na mkpebi pool/rotation (nyocha alive-status nke mkpebi scope-pool ji eme ihe ewepụlarị ya), a ga-emekwa ka ọ rụọ ọrụ ọzọ na-akpaghị aka ozugbo ọ malitere ịgafe probes ọzọ. Ọ na-eji otu threshold `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER`. Ọ bụrụ na flags abụọ ahụ bụ `true`, `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE` ga-emeri.                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTROL_PLANE_PROXY_DIRECT_FALLBACK`   | `false`                  | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                             | Kwe ka usoro OAuth na nkwado provider gafere proxy e kenyere kpọmkwem wee jikọọ ozugbo mgbe nyocha mbido nke nnweta proxy dara. Usoro ịdị-elu dị irè bụ mgbanwe Feature Flags DB > env var > ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_WAIT_MS`                          | `30000` (sekọnd 30)      | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                      | Oge kachasị elu a ga-echere na 429 tupu request ahụ ada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `RATE_LIMIT_EXECUTION_MAX_WAIT_MS`                | `600000` (nkeji 10)      | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                      | Oke kachasị elu nke oge request a nabatara nwere ike ịnọ na execution tupu rate-limit reservation ya agwụ — e kewapụrụ ya na mmefu oge ichere na queue ka fetch-start dị nwayọọ na gateways ndị na-abụghị incremental ghara ịkwụsị n'ihi oge (#12027).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_QUEUE_DEPTH`                      | `0` (agbanyụrụ)          | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                      | Oke nnabata queue: jiri 429 `queue_full` jụ request mgbe requests ruru ọnụ ọgụgụ a nọlarị na queue. `0` = enweghị oke (ndabara).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `RATE_LIMIT_AUTO_ENABLE`                          | _(edoghị)_               | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                      | Manye rate limit safety net nke auto-enable ka ọ dị on/off n'agbanyeghị ntọala Dashboard echekwara. Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`on` iji manye ya ka ọ dị on, ma ọ bụ `false`/`0`/`off` iji manye ya ka ọ dị off.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`                       | _(edoghị → off)_         | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                               | Nsochi cooldown zuru ụwa ọnụ nke provider/connection gafere requests, nke a na-ahọrọ ịbanye na ya. Ọ bụ OFF na ndabara (ọ na-ekpuchi otu mpaghara ahụ Connection Cooldown / Provider Circuit Breaker na-ekpuchi). Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`on` iji mee ka ọ rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MIN_MS`                        | `5000`                   | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                               | Cooldown kacha nta (ms) tupu a nwaa provider/connection dara ada ọzọ. A na-amụba ya n'usoro exponential dabere na ọdịda ndị na-esochi onwe ha. A na-eji ya naanị mgbe `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED` dị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MAX_MS`                        | `300000` (nkeji 5)       | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                               | Oke kachasị nke oge nkwụsịtụ (ms) tupu anwaa onye na-eweta ọrụ/njikọ dara ada ọzọ n'agbanyeghị ọnọdụ. A na-eji ya naanị mgbe `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED` nọ n'ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`                         | _(edoghị → gbanyụọ)_     | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (mbido) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (usoro ọrụ) | **Gịnị:** iweghachi iyi upstream e gbubiri n'ụzọ onye ọrụ na-agaghị ahụ (ọdụ free-claude-code). Ọ na-ejide windo SSE mmeghe ruo `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS` (750 ms), ka e wee meghee ma nwaa ọzọ n'ụzọ a na-adịghị ahụ anya ma ọ bụrụ na nkwụsị _tupu-commit_ — nke mere tupu byte ọ bụla erute onye ahịa. **Mgbe a ga-agbanye ya:** upstream ndị na-adịghị akwụsi ike/ndị na-ebikarị iyi 0-byte ná mmalite; hapụ ya ka ọ bụrụ GBANYỤỌ ma ọ bụrụ na ị gaghị anabata mgbakwunye oge ruru 750 ms tupu token mbụ apụta n'iyi ọ bụla. Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`on`. Ọ na-atọ ntọala Resilience echekwara; ozugbo edobere ntọala Dashboard, ọ bụ ya ga-emeri. |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_MIDSTREAM_ENABLED`               | _(edoghị → gbanyụọ)_     | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (mbido) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (usoro ọrụ) | **Gịnị:** ịga n'ihu n'etiti iyi (Ọkwa 4.4) — mgbe nkwụsị _mgbe-commit_ gasịrị (bytes erutela onye ahịa), ziga arịrịọ ọzọ jiri ederede ezughị ezu dị ka prefill nke onye enyemaka ma jikọta mgbakwunye fọdụrụ. Ọ bụ naanị maka iyi ederede nkịtị kwekọrọ na OpenAI; ọ naghị arụ ọrụ mgbe oku ngwaọrụ ka na-aga. **Mgbe a ga-agbanye ya:** mmepụta ogologo ndị a na-ebipụ n'etiti azịza ma ị nabata ka ọdụ e weghachiri bịarute n'otu ntabi anya kama token n'otu n'otu. Ọ nọọrọ onwe ya na `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED` (ogo ihe ize ndụ dị iche). Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`on`.                                                                                |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_ENABLED`              | _(edoghị → gbanyụọ)_     | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` → `open-sse/services/throughputWatchdog.ts`                 | Watchdog mmepụta bara uru nke iyi na-arụ ọrụ, nke a na-ahọrọ ịgbanye. Ọ na-achọpụta iyi ndị na-aga n'ihu izipu iberibe mana na-anọ n'okpuru ọsọ mmepụta onye enyemaka ahaziri; heartbeats, mmemme ojiji, delta efu, na usoro ngwaọrụ/ntụgharị uche anaghị eme ka à ga-asị na e nwere ọganihu. Ọ dị iche na oge nkwụsị n'ihi enweghị ọrụ na oge njedebe siri ike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WARMUP_MS`            | `30000`                  | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                   | Oge amara tupu nyocha throughput, nke amachibidoro n'etiti 0–600000 ms.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WINDOW_MS`            | `30000`                  | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                   | Windo mmepụta bara uru na-agagharị, nke amachibidoro n'etiti 1000–600000 ms; a chọrọ otu windo zuru ezu tupu ịkwụsị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_BYTES_PER_SECOND` | `4`                      | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                   | Ọsọ byte UTF-8 kacha nta kwa sekọnd maka mmepụta onye enyemaka (nnọchi token e ji akpachapụ anya), nke amachibidoro n'etiti 1–1000000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_USEFUL_BYTES`     | `1`                      | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                   | Nlele mmepụta bara uru kacha nta na-abụghị efu nke a na-ewere na a pụrụ ịtụ ya, nke amachibidoro n'etiti byte 1–1000000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS`                          | `3000`                   | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                | Oge nkewa (ms) n'etiti healthcheck token nke ndị na-eweta ọrụ mgbe usoro na-amalite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MIN_MS`                       | `500`                    | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                | Jitter enweghị usoro kacha nta (ms) a na-agbakwunye n'elu `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` n'etiti healthcheck token nke ndị na-eweta ọrụ, iji gbochie mgbawa arịrịọ (Nsogbu #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MAX_MS`                       | `5000`                   | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                | Jitter enweghị usoro kacha ukwuu (ms) a na-agbakwunye n'elu `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` n'etiti healthcheck token nke ndị na-eweta ọrụ, iji gbochie mgbawa arịrịọ (Nsogbu #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `HEALTHCHECK_BATCH_SIZE`                          | `20`                     | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                | Ogo nke otu nnyocha ndị a na-eme n'otu oge maka nyocha ahụike token mgbe usoro na-amalite; ụkpụrụ ndị ka ukwuu na-enyocha njikọ ndị ọzọ n'otu oge, ebe ụkpụrụ ndị ka nta na-ebelata ibu mberede (Nsogbu #7875, nlọghachi nke #7719).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `REQUEST_RETRY`                                   | `2`                      | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                     | Ọnụọgụ nnwale ọzọ akpaka a ga-eme mgbe nzaghachi oge-nkwụsị nke metụtara model gasịrị tupu eweghachitere onye ahịa njehie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `MAX_RETRY_INTERVAL_SEC`                          | `30`                     | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                     | Oke etiti oge nchere-na-abawanye (sekọnd) n'etiti nnwale oge-nkwụsị ndị ọzọ. A na-amachi ya n'ogo a n'agbanyeghị `Retry-After` sitere n'elu-usoro.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `HEADROOM_URL`                                    | `http://localhost:8787`  | `src/lib/headroom/detect.ts`                                                                 | URL proxy nchekwa-token Headroom. Usoro ndụ dashboard (`api/headroom/*`) na-ebido CLI `headroom-ai` mpaghara na loopback na ndabara; gbanwee ya naanị iji tụọ aka na proxy sidecar Docker dị na mpụga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

### Konstantị nhazi mgbake stream

A na-eji konstantị ndị e dere kpọmkwem n'ime koodu na
`open-sse/config/constants.ts` (`STREAM_RECOVERY`) hazie omume njigide mgbake, dịka egosiri ebe a maka ntụaka —
ịgbanwe ha chọrọ idezi koodu, ọ bụghị env var:

- `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS = 750` — ogologo oge a na-ejide windo SSE mmalite
  ka e wee nwee ike ịnwale ọzọ ma ọ bụrụ na ebipụ ya n'oge tupu e nyefee byte ọ bụla n'aka onye ahịa.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.BUFFER_MAX_BYTES = 65536` — oke siri ike maka windo a na-ejide; nyefee ya
  (flush + passthrough) ozugbo byte ruru ọnụọgụ a, n'agbanyeghị timer.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.EARLY_RETRY_MAX = 4` — oke mmepegharị stream sitere n'elu-usoro n'ụzọ na-apụtaghị ìhè
  mgbe a ka na-enyefebeghị njigide ahụ.

> **Oke ọnụego sliding-window maka provider ọ bụla (enweghị env var):** nke FCC wetara
> bụ _fallback_ oke ọnụego sliding-window maka provider ọ bụla dị na koodu
> (`open-sse/services/providerDefaultRateLimit.ts`, ejikọrọ site na
> `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`) mana ọ na-abịa na **map ndabara efu**
> ma **enweghị env var maka onye nchịkwa** ugbu a — a na-eme ka ọ rụọ ọrụ naanị site na test hook /
> ndezi koodu. E tinyereghị ya ụma na tebụl dị n'elu. Ihe mgbochi relay kwa-`(token, IP)`
> nke _nwere_ njikwa bụ `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE` (§3 Netwọk & Port).

---

## 22. Nchọpụta na mmezi njehie

> [!CAUTION]
> Ndị mgbanwe ndị a na-emepụta **mmepụta zuru ezu** ma nwee ike ikpughe data nzuzo. **Agbanyela ha mgbe ọ bụla na production.**

| Mgbanwe                               | Ndabara                               | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                                                     | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CURSOR_DEBUG`                        | _(edoghị ya)_                         | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Tọọ `1` iji gbanye ndekọ Cursor executor zuru ezu (iberibe SSE a gbanwere koodu ha, wdg.).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG`                 | _(edoghị ya)_                         | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Aha nnọchi nke `CURSOR_DEBUG` nke dakọtara na nsụgharị ochie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CURSOR_DUMP_FILE`                    | _(edoghị ya)_                         | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Ụzọ faịlụ nhọrọ nke na-anata iberibe Cursor raw a gbanwere koodu ha mgbe `CURSOR_DEBUG=1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `300000`                              | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Oge nkwụsị mgbe stream anaghị arụ ọrụ (ms) maka Cursor executor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CURSOR_KV_GRACE_MS`                  | `2000`                                | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Oghere amara (ms) mgbe composer kv_after_text soft terminator gasịrị ma ọ bụrụ na bytes ka dị na buffer — nke a na-enye trailing exec_mcp tool call oge iji mezue frame ya.                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CURSOR_TOOL_DIRECTIVE`               | agbanyere (`!== "0"`)                 | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Ntuziaka tool-commit nke na-eme ka composer-2.5 kpọọ tools n'ụzọ a pụrụ ịdabere na ya. Tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYSTEM_INSTRUCTION_APPEND` | _(edoghị ya)_                         | `open-sse/translator/request/claude-to-openai.ts`, `open-sse/translator/response/openai-to-claude.ts` | Ederede system prompt onye na-ahụ maka sistemụ kọwara nke a na-agbakwunye na system message MGBE ntụgharị gasịrị (ntinye mgbe ntụgharị gasịrị), na-erute ụzọ codex/Responses na `/v1/messages`. A na-ejikwa ya dị ka directive prefix a na-ewepụ na echoed system preamble blocks. Hapụ ya n'enweghị ntọala iji gbanyụọ ya. |
| `CURSOR_IMAGE_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `15000`                               | `open-sse/utils/cursorImages.ts`                                                                      | Oge nkwụsị nbudata maka onyonyo ọ bụla (ms) maka ntinye vision `image_url` dị anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CURSOR_STATE_DB_PATH`                | _(a na-enyocha)_                      | `open-sse/utils/cursorVersionDetector.ts`                                                             | Dochie nchọta state DB nke Cursor IDE a na-eji achọpụta nsụgharị IDE.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CURSOR_AGENT_CLI_VERSION`            | _(chọpụta / kpọgide)_                 | `open-sse/utils/cursorAgentCliVersion.ts`                                                             | ID build nke Agent CLI (`YYYY.MM.DD-<hash>`) maka `x-cursor-client-version: cli-…` na Agent Run.                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CURSOR_AGENT_BIN`                    | _(edoghị ya)_                         | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/cursorAgentImage.ts`                                     | Ụzọ gaa na binary Cursor Agent a na-eji emepụta onyonyo. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, handler ga-eji `providerSpecificData.agentBin`, mechaa PATH.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CURSOR_IMG_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `210000`                              | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/cursorAgentImage.ts`                                     | Oge ngụkọta kachasị maka onyonyo ọ bụla (ms) maka ọrụ onyonyo Cursor Agent.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CURSOR_IMG_MAX_CONCURRENT`           | `2`                                   | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/cursorAgentImage.ts`                                     | Ọnụ ụzọ concurrency nke shared-seat maka ọrụ onyonyo Cursor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CURSOR_IMG_MODEL`                    | arịrịọ / `auto`                       | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/cursorAgentImage.ts`                                     | Dochie `--model` nke Cursor CLI maka ọrụ onyonyo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `UC_IMAGE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`           | `2000`                                | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/ucImage.ts`                                              | Ugboro UC (uncensored.com) ji enyocha nsonaazụ mmepụta onyonyo (ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `UC_IMAGE_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `60000`                               | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/ucImage.ts`                                              | Oge ngụkọta kachasị maka nyocha nsonaazụ mmepụta onyonyo UC (ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `UC_VIDEO_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`           | `3000`                                | `open-sse/handlers/videoGeneration/providers/ucVideo.ts`                                              | Ugboro UC (uncensored.com) ji enyocha nsonaazụ mmepụta vidiyo (ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `UC_VIDEO_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `300000`                              | `open-sse/handlers/videoGeneration/providers/ucVideo.ts`                                              | Oge ngụkọta kachasị maka nyocha nsonaazụ mmepụta vidiyo UC (ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CURSOR_DATA_DIR`                     | _(a na-enyocha)_                      | `open-sse/utils/cursorAgentCliVersion.ts`                                                             | Dochie data dir nke Cursor Agent CLI (`…/versions/<id>`); ọ bụ otu var ahụ agent gọọmentị na-eji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CURSOR_TOKEN`                        | _(edoghị ya)_                         | `scripts/ad-hoc/cursor-tap.cjs`                                                                       | Token bearer Cursor kpọmkwem nke ngwa ndị mmepe na-eji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOG_REQUEST_SHAPE`         | agbanyụrụ (họrọ ịbanye site na `"1"`) | `src/app/api/v1/chat/completions/route.ts`                                                            | Dekọọ akara content-type/length maka nnukwu chat payloads mgbe edobere `"1"`. A na-agbanyụ ya na ndabara iji belata mkpọtụ ndekọ.                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `DEBUG_RESPONSES_SSE_TO_JSON`         | _(edoghị ya)_                         | `open-sse/handlers/responseTranslator.ts`                                                             | Tọọ `true` iji dekọọ nkọwa ntụgharị Responses API SSE→JSON.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `DEBUG_CLAUDE_NONSTREAM`              | _(edoghị ya)_                         | `open-sse/handlers/responseTranslator.ts`                                                             | Tọọ `true` iji gosipụta iberibe textContent efu n'ụzọ ntụgharị nzaghachi Claude (maka nchọpụta njehie naanị).                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_OMNIROUTE_E2E_MODE`      | _(edoghị ya)_                         | Ngwa ule E2E                                                                                          | Tọọ `true` iji gbanye ọnọdụ ule E2E (auth e mere ka ọ dị mfe, hooks ule).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

## 23. Njikọta GitHub

Kwe ka ndị ọrụ nwee ike ịkọ nsogbu ozugbo site na Dashboard.

| Ihe Mgbanwe           | Ndabara      | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                 | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                              |
| --------------------- | ------------ | ------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_REPO`  | _(edobeghị)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`           | Ebe nchekwa n'ụdị `owner/repo`.                                                                                                                    |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN` | _(edobeghị)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`           | GitHub Personal Access Token nwere ikike `issues:write`.                                                                                           |
| `GITHUB_TOKEN`        | _(edobeghị)_ | nhazi mbido nke nsogbu / ihe enyemaka cloud agent | Token nnweta GitHub izugbe a na-eji dị ka ndabere maka `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN`, nke ihe enyemaka cloud agent dị na `src/lib/cloudAgent/*` na-ejikwa. |

---

## Ọnọdụ Mbugharị

Maka nduzi SRE nke backend relay (omume ts/bifrost/auto, ebe a ga-edobe 9router ma e jiri ya tụnyere CLIProxyAPI, na atụmatụ ndabere maka oke mbufe), lee [Atụmatụ Backend Relay](/docs/reference/RELAY_BACKEND_STRATEGY.md).

### Mmepe Obodo Dịkarịsịrị Mfe

```bash
JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)
API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)
INITIAL_PASSWORD=dev123
PORT=20128
NODE_ENV=development
```

### Mmepụta Docker

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<generated>
API_KEY_SECRET=<generated>
INITIAL_PASSWORD=<generated>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<generated>
DATA_DIR=/data
PORT=20128
API_PORT=20129
NODE_ENV=production
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://localhost:20128
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=8192
CORS_ORIGIN=https://your-frontend.example.com
```

### Sistemụ Na-enweghị Njikọ Ịntanetị / CI

```bash
JWT_SECRET=test-jwt-secret-for-ci
API_KEY_SECRET=test-api-key-secret-for-ci
INITIAL_PASSWORD=testpass
NODE_ENV=production
OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES=true
APP_LOG_TO_FILE=false
```

### VPS nwere Reverse Proxy (nginx + Cloudflare)

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<generated>
API_KEY_SECRET=<generated>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<generated>
PORT=20128
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:20128
CORS_ORIGIN=https://omniroute.example.com
ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true
CLI_COMPAT_ALL=1
```

---

## 24. Ebe Nnwale Nchekwa Skills (v3.8.0+)

Oke na ntọala nchekwa ndị a na-etinye mgbe framework Skills (`src/lib/skills/`) na-eme automation ndị ọrụ kọwara n'ime gburugburu e kewapụrụ maka nnwale.

| Ihe Mgbanwe                       | Ndabara                                               | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                  | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| --------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `10000` (10 s)                                        | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Oke oge wall-clock maka oge mmejuputa nke ọ bụla nke koodu skill dị na sandbox. Nke a bụ oke siri ike; a ga-akwụsị ihe ọ bụla gafere ya.                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SKILLS_EXECUTION_TIMEOUT_MS`     | _(na-eji `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`` dị ka ndabere)_ | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Oke oge maka nhazi mmejuputa skill n'ogo dị elu. Tọọ ya karịa `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS` iji kwe ka usoro ọrụ nwere ọtụtụ nzọụkwụ rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SKILLS_MAX_FILE_BYTES`           | `1048576` (1 MB)                                      | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ọnụ byte kachasị skill nwere ike ịgụ site na otu faịlụ ọ bụla dị na sandbox.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `SKILLS_MAX_HTTP_RESPONSE_BYTES`  | `256000` (250 KB)                                     | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ọnụ byte kachasị a ga-edekọ site na otu nzaghachi HTTP ọ bụla n'ime skill.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SKILLS_MAX_SANDBOX_OUTPUT_CHARS` | `100000`                                              | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Oke siri ike nke mkpụrụedemede stdout/stderr a na-eweghachi site na otu ọkpụkpọ sandbox.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED`  | `false`                                               | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Tọọ `1`/`true` iji kwe ka njikọ netwọk na-apụ apụ site n'ime sandbox. Ndabara ya bụ **nkewapụrụ** maka nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SKILLS_ALLOWED_SANDBOX_IMAGES`   | _(efu)_                                               | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ndepụta onyonyo container ndị e kwere maka mmejuputa sandbox, nke rikoma kewara. Ọ bụrụ na ọ tọgbọ chakoo, ọ pụtara na ọ bụ naanị ndabara arụnyere n'ime ka a ga-eji.                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_DOCKER_IMAGE`     | _(ndabara arụnyere n'ime)_                            | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Onyonyo container a na-eji mgbe a na-emepụta sandbox Docker na-akwado. Gbanwee ya iji kpọchie onyonyo ntọala omenala e wusiri ike.                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_RUNTIME`          | `auto`                                                | `src/lib/skills/sandbox.ts`, `src/lib/skills/containerProvider.ts` | Runtime container maka idobe skill na sandbox: `auto` \| `docker` \| `apple` \| `wsl` \| `orbstack` \| `podman`. `auto` na-ahọrọ runtime arụnyere kacha mma dabere na OS nke host (Apple Container/OrbStack na macOS, WSL Container na Windows, Podman na Linux), ma na-eji Docker dị ka ndabere ma ọ bụrụ na ndị ahụ adịghị. |

> [!CAUTION]
> Ime ka `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED=true` rụọ ọrụ na-emepe ụzọ egress site na koodu skill ọ bụla. Jikọtara ya na `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED=true` yana amụma `CORS_ORIGIN`/proxy siri ike n'ebe mbugote ọtụtụ ndị na-eji ọnụ.

## 25. Oke Ndị Na-eweta Ọrụ, Ọwara Netwọk, Nkwado Ndabere & Ihe Ndị Ọzọ nke Oge Ọrụ

Ebe njedebe oke ndị na-eweta ọrụ, ọwara netwọk (Tailscale, Ngrok, MITM debug proxy), ọdọ ụzọ ọpụpụ 1Proxy, nkwado ndabere nke ebe nchekwa data, na obere mgbanwe ntọala maka njirimara ọ bụla nke oyi akwa executor ma ọ bụ scripts na-eji.

| Variable                                       | Default                                                                     | Source File                                                               | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `REDIS_URL`                                    | `redis://localhost:6379`                                                    | `src/shared/utils/rateLimiter.ts`                                         | Eriri njikọ Redis maka backend nke rate limiter.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_HOST`                     | _(host mmepụta)_                                                            | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Gbanwee host a na-eji enweta oke coding-plan nke Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_QUOTA_URL`                | ewepụtara site na host                                                      | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Mgbanwe URL oke zuru ezu maka Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_COOKIE`                            | _(edobeghị)_                                                                | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Cookie nnọkọ console maka ụzọ mbata oke Token Plan nkeonwe nke Qwen Cloud / Model Studio (igodo API inference enweghị ike ịgụ ya). Detuo isi arịrịọ `Cookie` dum — ọ nwere `login_qwencloud_ticket` — site na oku `api.json` ọ bụla gaa na `cs-data.qwencloud.com` na home.qwencloud.com › Billing › Subscription (F12 › Network). Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo ma bụrụ nke nnọkọ kpọmkwem; họrọ mpaghara Dashboard `qwenCloudCookie` nke njikọ ọ bụla.                                                                                                  |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_SEC_TOKEN`                         | _(edobeghị)_                                                                | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Mgbanwe aka nke `sec_token` maka ụzọ mbata console Token Plan. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; mgbe edobeghị ya, fetcher na-enweta ya site na HTML dashboard site n'iji cookie ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_HOST`                         | `https://cs-data.qwencloud.com`                                             | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Mgbanwe host nke ụzọ mbata maka fetcher oke Token Plan nkeonwe (dịka `bailian-singapore-cs.alibabacloud.com` maka console Model Studio).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_DASHBOARD_URL`                | `https://home.qwencloud.com/`                                               | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | URL dashboard a na-eji enweta `sec_token` site na HTML nke abanyelarị na ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_VISION_FE_PATH`             | `/costing-balance/free-quota-image-video`                                   | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierQuotaFetcher.ts`                        | Mgbanwe ụzọ front-end console maka inweta oke vision/media nke free-tier Alibaba Model Studio.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_MULTIMODAL_FE_PATH`         | `/costing-balance/free-quota-multimodal`                                    | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierQuotaFetcher.ts`                        | Mgbanwe ụzọ front-end nke console maka iweta oke ojiji multimodal nke ọkwa efu Alibaba Model Studio.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_AUDIO_FE_PATH`              | `/costing-balance/free-quota-audio`                                         | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierQuotaFetcher.ts`                        | Mgbanwe ụzọ front-end nke console maka iweta oke ojiji ọdịyo nke ọkwa efu Alibaba Model Studio.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_ALLOWLIST_PATH`             | _(edobeghị)_                                                                | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierAllowlist.ts`                           | Ụzọ nhọrọ gaa na faịlụ JSON dị na kọmputa nke na-anọchi ndepụta ụdịdị ederede Alibaba nke ọkwa efu e wuru n'ime ya. Ọ bụrụ na ọ dịghị, ọ ga-eji `$DATA_DIR/alibaba-free-tier-allowlist.json`, ma mesịa `config/alibaba-free-tier-allowlist.json`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CONTEXT_RESERVE_TOKENS`                       | `1024`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/contextManager.ts`                                     | Token ndị e debere maka mmepụta mmecha mgbe a na-agbakọ oke token nke prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CONTEXT_KEEP_LATEST_IMAGES`                   | `2`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/contextManager.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ onyonyo inline kachasị ọhụrụ a ga-edowe mgbe a na-ewepụ ndị ochie ka ha daba na windo context (#8560).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `MODEL_ALIAS_COMPAT_ENABLED`                   | agbanyere                                                                   | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                                              | Gbanye ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ oyi akwa ndakọrịta model-alias ochie nke ndị ahịa ochie na-eji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_EMERGENCY_FALLBACK`                 | agbanyere                                                                   | `open-sse/services/emergencyFallback.ts`                                  | Tọọ `false` (ma ọ bụ `0`) iji gbanyụọ ụzọ ndabere mberede mgbe mmefu ego gwụsịrị, nke na-atụgharị arịrịọ ndị dara ada gaa na model efu `nvidia`/`openai/gpt-oss-120b`. Usoro ibu ụzọ dị irè bụ mgbanwe Feature Flags DB > env var > ndabara; ọ bụrụ na ọ dịghị, ọrụ ahụ ga-eji uru env ahụ ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `COMMAND_CODE_CALLBACK_PORT`                   | _(edobeghị)_                                                                | `src/app/api/providers/command-code/auth/shared.ts`                       | Port mpaghara eji maka callback ụdị OAuth sitere na enyemaka Command Code CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `COMMAND_CODE_VERSION`                         | `0.33.2`                                                                    | `open-sse/executors/commandCode.ts`                                       | Uru a na-eziga dị ka header `x-command-code-version` gaa na Command Code upstream. Gbanwee ya iji bulie ụdị CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `COMMANDCODE_API_URL`                          | `https://api.commandcode.ai`                                                | `open-sse/services/usage/command-code.ts`                                 | URL ntọala maka Command Code usage/quota upstream nke telemetry nke smartphone quota-fetcher na-eji. Gbanwee ya maka Command Code API a na-akwado n'onwe gị/ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `MITM_LOCAL_PORT`                              | `443`                                                                       | `src/mitm/server.cjs`                                                     | Port bind mpaghara maka proxy ndozi nsogbu MITM.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `MITM_DISABLE_TLS_VERIFY`                      | `0`                                                                         | `src/mitm/server.cjs`                                                     | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji gbanyụọ nkwenye TLS nke upstream (maka mmepe naanị).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `MITM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | `60000`                                                                     | `src/mitm/socketTimeouts.ts`, `src/mitm/server.cjs`                       | Oge nkwụsị socket na-adịghị arụ ọrụ (ms) maka njikọ ndị e tinyere na proxy; a na-akwụsị socket ndị gafere oge a iji gbochie ọwara ndị mepere naanị otu akụkụ ịgbapụta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `MITM_VERBOSE`                                 | `1`                                                                         | `src/mitm/server.cjs`, `src/mitm/_internal/bypass.cjs`                    | Ogo nkọwa ndekọ mkpebi ụzọ: `0` na-emechi ndekọ, uru ndị dị elu na-edekọ ọtụtụ mkpebi ngafe/ụzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `MITM_ROOT_CA_ENABLED`                         | `false`                                                                     | `src/mitm/manager.ts`                                                     | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji họrọ ụdị root-CA + asambodo leaf nke onye nnabata ọ bụla (#6684). Nrụnye ọhụrụ na-enweta ya na-akpaghị aka; nrụnye ndị nwere asambodo leaf legacy a tụkwasịrị obi na mbụ na-edobe asambodo fixed-SAN legacy ma ọ bụrụ na ahọrọghị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MITM_CERT_MODE`                               | `legacy`                                                                    | `src/mitm/manager.ts`, `src/mitm/server.cjs`                              | Onye njikwa MITM na-edobe ya MAKA usoro proxy a malitere (`root-ca` \| `legacy`) — ọ na-egosipụta mkpebi mbugharị asambodo; e kwesịghị iji aka debe ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_NO_SUDO`                            | `0`                                                                         | `src/mitm/systemCommands.ts`                                              | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` (eziokwu) iji wepụ `sudo` dị n'ihu n'iwu ntụkwasị-obi asambodo MITM — maka nrụnye na-enweghị root / nke namespace onye ọrụ ebe onye na-arụ ọrụ ji aka tụkwasị CA obi (dịka ọmụmaatụ site na usoro extra-CA-certs nke Node).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
